# cmgo core Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `cmgo`, a single Go binary that serves context-mode's 5 core MCP tools (`ctx_execute`, `ctx_execute_file`, `ctx_batch_execute`, `ctx_search`, `ctx_fetch_and_index`) to Claude Code. Output must match the TypeScript fork, while using about 5× less memory and writing nothing to disk when idle.

**Architecture:**
- One `cmgo serve` process per Claude Code session, speaking MCP over stdio. All processes share one SQLite file (WAL, FTS5 porter + trigram) at `~/.claude/cmgo/cmgo.db`.
- Packages are layered: `config`, `store`, `security`, `executor`, `fetch`, then `mcpserver`, which registers the tools and formats the TS-identical output, then `cmd/cmgo`.
- Behavior is ported from the TS fork at commit `a5e6dd31`. Parity is proven by golden files generated from the TS code.

**Tech Stack:**
- Go 1.27.2
- `github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk` v1.8.0
- `github.com/ncruces/go-sqlite3` v0.35.6 (no cgo, FTS5)
- `github.com/JohannesKaufmann/html-to-markdown/v2` v2.5.2
- Python 3 for the acceptance scripts
- Node, only to regenerate golden files by hand

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-cmgo-core-design.md` (sub-project 1 of 5). Read it alongside this plan. Spec §2.2 lists every intentional difference from TS.

**How the code in this plan was produced:** every code block was built and tested in a draft module before it was copied in here, verbatim. The assembled plan was then replayed task by task, Task 1 through 35, into an empty module, with `go vet` and that task's package tests run after each task. Every task passed. At the end: 184 tests passed, 0 failed, `-race` clean, on macOS arm64. Appendix B lists what the replay did not exercise. Each code block is the complete file named on the line above it. When a later task shows a file that an earlier task created, replace the whole file.

## Global Constraints

- **Repo and module:** the repo is `~/GolandProjects/github.com/lang315/context-mode-go`. The module is `github.com/lang315/context-mode-go`. `go.mod` says `go 1.27.2`.
- **Platforms:** macOS and Linux only. Write no Windows code paths. Verified on macOS arm64 only.
- **Dependencies:** go-sdk v1.8.0; ncruces/go-sqlite3 v0.35.6, with FTS5 enabled via `driver.Open(dsn, fts5.Register)`; html-to-markdown/v2 v2.5.2.
  - No cgo, and add no other dependencies.
  - If a build reports `missing go.sum entry`, run `go mod tidy`.
- **Data location:** `$CMGO_DIR/cmgo.db`, where `CMGO_DIR` defaults to `~/.claude/cmgo`.
  - Never write to `~/.claude/context-mode`, to the real `~/.claude` from tests, or to the TS repo.
  - Tests use `t.TempDir()`.
- **Project dir:** `CMGO_PROJECT_DIR`, then `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`, then the current working directory. It is stored lowercased on darwin.
- **Parity with TS:** tool names, parameter names, defaults, thresholds, labels and output strings match TS `a5e6dd31`, except the rows listed in spec §2.2.
- **Constants:**

  | Constant | Value |
  |---|---|
  | `intent` search | only for output over 5000 B |
  | Index instead of return | output over 102400 B |
  | Output hard cap | 100 MB, then the process group is killed |
  | Chunk size | 4096 B max |
  | Fetch | 30 s, 50 MB, 24 h TTL, 5 redirects |
  | Source retention | 14 days; cleanup at most once per 24 h |
  | Ranking | `RRF_K` 60, `bm25(chunks, 5.0, 1.0)` |
  | `ctx_search` effective limit | `min(limit, 2)`; flood guard: 60 s window, soft cap after 3 calls, block after 8 |

- **Tool registration:** use the low-level `s.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{…, InputSchema: json.RawMessage(...)}, handler)` with a hand-written schema, never the generic `mcp.AddTool`. Descriptions are copied verbatim from TS, minus features cmgo lacks.
- **Tool results:** build them only with `TextResult`, `ErrorResult` or `ArgError`. These replace invalid UTF-8 with U+FFFD.
- **Disk writes:** each process keeps one DB connection, and no timer touches the disk. Idle writes must stay at or below 0.1 MB/min.
- **Process control:** `Setpgid` + `cmd.Cancel` (SIGKILL to `-pgid`). `WaitDelay` is 2 s for foreground runs and 0 for background runs.
- **Commits:** Conventional Commits, in English, one per task (the task's last step).

## Review Focus

These are the five conditions most likely to hurt someone using cmgo that the spec implies but does not list. Each has a test in the task that owns the code.

1. **A session starts while another session is writing the shared DB.** It must start normally, not exit with `database is locked`. Task 2's `TestOpenThenCleanupConcurrently` reproduces a real bug found while writing it: the WAL pragma was applied per connection, so the second session failed in round 0. `ensureWAL` fixes it. `TestOpenConcurrentFreshDB` and `TestOpenWhileAnotherConnectionWrites` are guards that already passed before the fix. Task 31 adds `TestServeConcurrentSessionsSameDB` at the `serve` level.
2. **Claude Code closes the session while a foreground command is still running.** The command and its children must be gone after shutdown (`TestServeShutdownKillsForegroundRun`).
3. **`PATH` entries or project paths contain a single quote, spaces or non-ASCII.** Scripts still run, with the right cwd and the right `FILE_CONTENT` (`TestShellPathWithSingleQuote`, `TestProjectDirWithSpacesQuotesUnicode`).
4. **Raw text pasted into `ctx_search`:** FTS5 operators, unbalanced quotes, column filters, only stopwords, SQL-looking text. This must never surface an SQL error (`TestSearchSurvivesFTSSyntax`).
5. **Binary output, or a single multi-MB line, from `ctx_execute`.** It comes back as valid UTF-8, gets indexed, and stays searchable (`TestBinaryAndLongLineOutput`, `TestExecuteBinaryOutputIsValidUTF8`).

## File Structure

| Path | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `cmd/cmgo/main.go`, `serve.go` | Subcommand dispatch; `serve` wires config, store, executor, policy, fetcher and the MCP server over stdio |
| `internal/config` | Paths and environment (`CMGO_DIR`, project dir, strict fetch) |
| `internal/store` | SQLite schema and migrations, `ensureWAL`, retention, the 3 chunkers (Markdown, plain text, JSON), `Index` + vocabulary, porter/trigram RRF search, rerank, fuzzy fallback, timeline sort, snippets, distinctive terms |
| `internal/security` | Settings loading and cache, `Bash()` deny matching across chains and subshells, embedded-command extraction for non-shell code, the `Read()` and project-boundary checks |
| `internal/executor` | Runtime detection for 12 languages, script preparation (wrappers, `FILE_CONTENT` prefixes, `PATH` line), env denylist, spawn/timeout/cap/process-group kill, background runs |
| `internal/fetch` | `ClassifyIP` and the dial-time SSRF guard, redirects, size caps, JSON/HTML/text routing, HTML to Markdown |
| `internal/mcpserver` | `Deps`, argument coercion (TS zod preprocessors), results; one file per tool; flood guard; test harness |
| `bench/` | Acceptance scripts for spec criteria 1, 2 and 4 (Python, macOS `proc_pid_rusage`) |
| `testdata/corpus`, `testdata/golden`, `tools/golden-gen` | Golden parity corpus, TS-generated expected output, and the generator (spec criterion 3) |

package config

import (
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"testing"
)

func env(m map[string]string) func(string) string {
	return func(k string) string { return m[k] }
}

func TestLoadDefaults(t *testing.T) {
	c, err := Load(env(map[string]string{"HOME": "/h"}), func() (string, error) { return "/work/Proj", nil })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if c.DataDir != "/h/.claude/cmgo" || c.DBPath != "/h/.claude/cmgo/cmgo.db" {
		t.Fatalf("data dir: %+v", c)
	}
	if c.ProjectDir != "/work/Proj" {
		t.Fatalf("project dir: %q", c.ProjectDir)
	}
	want := "/work/Proj"
	if runtime.GOOS == "darwin" {
		want = "/work/proj"
	}
	if c.ProjectKey != want {
		t.Fatalf("project key: %q want %q", c.ProjectKey, want)
	}
	if c.FetchStrict {
		t.Fatal("strict should default off")
	}
}

func TestLoadOverridesInPriorityOrder(t *testing.T) {
	c, err := Load(env(map[string]string{
		"HOME": "/h", "CMGO_DIR": "/d", "CMGO_PROJECT_DIR": "/a", "CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR": "/b", "CMGO_FETCH_STRICT": "1",
	}), func() (string, error) { return "/c", nil })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if c.DataDir != "/d" || c.ProjectDir != "/a" || !c.FetchStrict {
		t.Fatalf("%+v", c)
	}
	c, _ = Load(env(map[string]string{"HOME": "/h", "CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR": "/b"}), func() (string, error) { return "/c", nil })
	if c.ProjectDir != "/b" {
		t.Fatalf("CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR should win over cwd: %q", c.ProjectDir)
	}
}

func TestLoadMakesRelativeProjectAbsolute(t *testing.T) {
	c, _ := Load(env(map[string]string{"HOME": "/h", "CMGO_PROJECT_DIR": "rel"}), func() (string, error) { return "/c", nil })
	if !filepath.IsAbs(c.ProjectDir) {
		t.Fatalf("not absolute: %q", c.ProjectDir)
	}
}

`internal/config/config.go`:

```go
// Package config resolves cmgo's paths and environment switches.
package config

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
)

// Config is the process-wide configuration, resolved once at startup.
type Config struct {
	DataDir     string // $CMGO_DIR, default ~/.claude/cmgo
	DBPath      string // DataDir/cmgo.db
	ProjectDir  string // absolute project dir, as given (used as cwd)
	ProjectKey  string // NormalizeProject(ProjectDir), stored in sources.project
	Home        string
	FetchStrict bool // CMGO_FETCH_STRICT=1 blocks private ranges
}

// Load reads the environment. getenv and getwd are injectable for tests.
func Load(getenv func(string) string, getwd func() (string, error)) (Config, error) {
	home := getenv("HOME")
	if home == "" {
		h, err := os.UserHomeDir()
		if err != nil {
			return Config{}, err
		}
		home = h
	}
	dataDir := getenv("CMGO_DIR")
	if dataDir == "" {
		dataDir = filepath.Join(home, ".claude", "cmgo")
	}
	project := getenv("CMGO_PROJECT_DIR")
	if project == "" {
		project = getenv("CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")
	}
	if project == "" {
		wd, err := getwd()
		if err != nil {
			return Config{}, err
		}
		project = wd
	}
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(project)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	return Config{
		DataDir:     dataDir,
		DBPath:      filepath.Join(dataDir, "cmgo.db"),
		ProjectDir:  abs,
		ProjectKey:  NormalizeProject(abs),
		Home:        home,
		FetchStrict: getenv("CMGO_FETCH_STRICT") == "1",
	}, nil
}

// NormalizeProject cleans an absolute path; darwin paths are lowercased
// because the default APFS volume is case-insensitive (TS parity).
func NormalizeProject(abs string) string {
	p := filepath.Clean(abs)
	if runtime.GOOS == "darwin" {
		p = strings.ToLower(p)
	}
	return p
}
```

`cmd/cmgo/main.go`:

```go
// Command cmgo is a Go rewrite of context-mode for Claude Code.
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
)

// version is set at build time: go build -ldflags "-X main.version=v0.1.0".
var version = "dev"

func main() {
	os.Exit(run(os.Args[1:], os.Stdout, os.Stderr))
}

func run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int {
	if len(args) == 0 {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "usage: cmgo <serve|version>")
		return 2
	}
	switch args[0] {
	case "version", "--version":
		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "cmgo "+version)
		return 0
	default:
		fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "cmgo: unknown command %q\n", args[0])
		return 2
	}
}
```

`internal/store/store_test.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func openTest(t *testing.T) *Store {
	t.Helper()
	s, err := Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "sub", "cmgo.db"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { s.Close() })
	return s
}

func TestOpenSchemaAndPragmas(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "a", "b", "cmgo.db")
	s, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var v int
	var jm string
	var busy, fk, syncMode int
	s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA user_version").Scan(&v)
	s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA journal_mode").Scan(&jm)
	s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA busy_timeout").Scan(&busy)
	s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA foreign_keys").Scan(&fk)
	s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA synchronous").Scan(&syncMode)
	if v != 1 || jm != "wal" || busy != 30000 || fk != 1 || syncMode != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("user_version=%d journal=%s busy=%d fk=%d sync=%d", v, jm, busy, fk, syncMode)
	}
	for _, tbl := range []string{"meta", "sources", "chunks", "chunks_trigram", "vocabulary"} {
		var n int
		if err := s.db.QueryRow("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = ?", tbl).Scan(&n); err != nil || n != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("table %s: n=%d err=%v", tbl, n, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := s.db.Exec("INSERT INTO sources(project, label, chunk_count, code_chunk_count) VALUES('p','x',0,0)"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s.Close()

	// Reopen: migrations are not re-run and data survives.
	s, err = Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer s.Close()
	var n int
	s.db.QueryRow("SELECT count(*) FROM sources").Scan(&n)
	if n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("sources after reopen = %d", n)
	}
}

func TestCleanupIfDue(t *testing.T) {
	s := openTest(t)
	exec := func(q string, args ...any) {
		t.Helper()
		if _, err := s.db.Exec(q, args...); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	exec("INSERT INTO sources(id, project, label, chunk_count, code_chunk_count, indexed_at) VALUES(1,'p','old',1,0,'2020-01-01 00:00:00')")
	exec("INSERT INTO sources(id, project, label, chunk_count, code_chunk_count) VALUES(2,'q','fresh',1,0)")
	for _, tbl := range []string{"chunks", "chunks_trigram"} {
		exec("INSERT INTO " + tbl + "(title, content, source_id, content_type) VALUES('t','old body',1,'prose')")
		exec("INSERT INTO " + tbl + "(title, content, source_id, content_type) VALUES('t','fresh body',2,'prose')")
	}

	now := time.Now()
	const day = 24 * time.Hour
	n, err := s.CleanupIfDue(now, 14*day, day)
	if err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("first cleanup n=%d err=%v", n, err)
	}
	var left, chunks, tri int
	s.db.QueryRow("SELECT count(*) FROM sources").Scan(&left)
	s.db.QueryRow("SELECT count(*) FROM chunks").Scan(&chunks)
	s.db.QueryRow("SELECT count(*) FROM chunks_trigram").Scan(&tri)
	if left != 1 || chunks != 1 || tri != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("after cleanup sources=%d chunks=%d trigram=%d", left, chunks, tri)
	}

	// Not due again within 24h, even if an old source appears.
	exec("UPDATE sources SET indexed_at = '2020-01-01 00:00:00' WHERE id = 2")
	if n, err := s.CleanupIfDue(now.Add(23*time.Hour), 14*day, day); err != nil || n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("second cleanup n=%d err=%v", n, err)
	}
	// Due after 24h.
	if n, err := s.CleanupIfDue(now.Add(25*time.Hour), 14*day, day); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("third cleanup n=%d err=%v", n, err)
	}
	var last string
	s.db.QueryRow("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key='last_cleanup'").Scan(&last)
	if last != now.Add(25*time.Hour).UTC().Format(time.RFC3339) {
		t.Fatalf("last_cleanup = %q", last)
	}
}

// A not-due CleanupIfDue must only read. Another Store holds the write lock;
// the not-due call must return at once, while a due call (control) waits.
func TestCleanupNotDueTakesNoWriteLock(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "cmgo.db")
	s, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer s.Close()
	other, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer other.Close()

	now := time.Now()
	const day = 24 * time.Hour
	if _, err := s.CleanupIfDue(now, 14*day, day); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	lock, err := other.db.Begin() // BEGIN IMMEDIATE: holds the write lock
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { _, err := s.CleanupIfDue(now.Add(time.Hour), 14*day, day); done <- err }()
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
		lock.Rollback()
		t.Fatal("not-due CleanupIfDue waited for the write lock")
	}

	// Control: a due call needs the lock, so it waits until the lock is released.
	go func() { _, err := s.CleanupIfDue(now.Add(25*time.Hour), 14*day, day); done <- err }()
	select {
	case <-done:
		lock.Rollback()
		t.Fatal("due CleanupIfDue did not wait for the write lock")
	case <-time.After(300 * time.Millisecond):
	}
	lock.Rollback()
	if err := <-done; err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}
```

`internal/store/open_concurrent_test.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

// Review focus: several Claude sessions starting at once open the same fresh
// DB. The WAL switch needs an exclusive lock and SQLite returns SQLITE_BUSY
// for it without consulting busy_timeout, so Open must retry.
func TestOpenConcurrentFreshDB(t *testing.T) {
	for round := 0; round < 5; round++ {
		path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "cmgo.db")
		const n = 8
		var wg sync.WaitGroup
		errs := make(chan error, n)
		stores := make(chan *Store, n)
		start := make(chan struct{})
		for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
			wg.Add(1)
			go func() {
				defer wg.Done()
				<-start
				s, err := Open(path)
				if err != nil {
					errs <- err
					return
				}
				stores <- s
			}()
		}
		close(start)
		wg.Wait()
		close(errs)
		close(stores)
		for err := range errs {
			t.Fatalf("round %d: concurrent Open failed: %v", round, err)
		}
		for s := range stores {
			var mode string
			if err := s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA journal_mode").Scan(&mode); err != nil || mode != "wal" {
				t.Fatalf("journal_mode = %q, %v", mode, err)
			}
			s.Close()
		}
	}
}

// Review focus: a new session starts while another session holds a write
// transaction (indexing). Open must wait for the lock instead of failing.
func TestOpenWhileAnotherConnectionWrites(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "cmgo.db")
	s1, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer s1.Close()
	tx, err := s1.db.Begin() // _txlock=immediate: takes the write lock now
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := tx.Exec("INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES ('hold', '1')"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	released := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
		tx.Commit()
		close(released)
	}()
	s2, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Open during another connection's write failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer s2.Close()
	<-released
}

// Review focus: the exact `cmgo serve` startup sequence (Open, then
// CleanupIfDue, which writes on a fresh DB) run by two sessions at once.
// Before the WAL switch moved out of the DSN this failed in round 0 with
// "invalid _pragma: database is locked".
func TestOpenThenCleanupConcurrently(t *testing.T) {
	for round := 0; round < 50; round++ {
		path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "cmgo.db")
		var wg sync.WaitGroup
		errs := make(chan error, 4)
		for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
			wg.Add(1)
			go func() {
				defer wg.Done()
				s, err := Open(path)
				if err != nil {
					errs <- err
					return
				}
				defer s.Close()
				if _, err := s.CleanupIfDue(time.Now(), 14*24*time.Hour, 24*time.Hour); err != nil {
					errs <- err
				}
			}()
		}
		wg.Wait()
		close(errs)
		for err := range errs {
			t.Fatalf("round %d: %v", round, err)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/store/store.go`:

```go
// Package store is the SQLite FTS5 knowledge base: schema, chunkers, search
// and snippets. Port of context-mode src/store.ts (ST) with the differences
// listed in the cmgo core spec §2.2 and §3.
package store

import (
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/ncruces/go-sqlite3"
	"github.com/ncruces/go-sqlite3/driver"
	"github.com/ncruces/go-sqlite3/ext/fts5"
)

// ContentType is the chunk kind stored in the content_type column.
type ContentType string

const (
	Code  ContentType = "code"
	Prose ContentType = "prose"
)

// sqliteTime is the layout of datetime('now') values in sources.indexed_at.
const sqliteTime = "2006-01-02 15:04:05"

// Store owns one *sql.DB with a single connection kept open for the life of
// the process.
type Store struct {
	db *sql.DB

	mu         sync.Mutex
	indexCalls int               // Index calls in this process; FTS optimize every 50 (ST:1132)
	fuzzyKeys  []string          // LRU order of fuzzyVals, oldest first (ST:470)
	fuzzyVals  map[string]string // word -> correction; "" means no correction
}

// migrations[i] moves the schema from user_version i to i+1.
var migrations = []string{
	`CREATE TABLE meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE sources (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  project TEXT NOT NULL,
  label TEXT NOT NULL,
  chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
  code_chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
  indexed_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  UNIQUE (project, label)
);
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE chunks USING fts5(
  title, content, source_id UNINDEXED, content_type UNINDEXED,
  tokenize = 'porter unicode61');
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE chunks_trigram USING fts5(
  title, content, source_id UNINDEXED, content_type UNINDEXED,
  tokenize = 'trigram');
CREATE TABLE vocabulary (word TEXT PRIMARY KEY);`,
}

// Open creates the parent directory, opens the DB with WAL pragmas and
// applies pending migrations.
func Open(path string) (*Store, error) {
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	// journal_mode is not in the DSN: WAL persists in the file, and switching
	// to it needs an exclusive lock that SQLite refuses with SQLITE_BUSY
	// without consulting busy_timeout. Re-applying it on every connection made
	// a session fail to start while another session was writing.
	dsn := (&url.URL{Scheme: "file", Path: abs}).String() +
		"?_txlock=immediate" +
		"&_pragma=busy_timeout(30000)" +
		"&_pragma=synchronous(NORMAL)" +
		"&_pragma=foreign_keys(ON)"
	db, err := driver.Open(dsn, fts5.Register)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	db.SetMaxOpenConns(1)
	db.SetConnMaxLifetime(0)
	db.SetConnMaxIdleTime(0)
	s := &Store{db: db, fuzzyVals: map[string]string{}}
	if err := s.ensureWAL(); err != nil {
		db.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := s.migrate(); err != nil {
		db.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	return s, nil
}

// walSwitchTimeout bounds retries of the WAL switch; it matches busy_timeout.
const walSwitchTimeout = 30 * time.Second

// ensureWAL switches the file to WAL once. Reading the mode takes no lock;
// the switch is retried with backoff while another connection holds a lock.
func (s *Store) ensureWAL() error {
	deadline := time.Now().Add(walSwitchTimeout)
	wait := 10 * time.Millisecond
	for {
		var mode string
		err := s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA journal_mode").Scan(&mode)
		if err == nil && mode == "wal" {
			return nil
		}
		if err == nil {
			err = s.db.QueryRow("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL").Scan(&mode)
			if err == nil && mode == "wal" {
				return nil
			}
			if err == nil {
				err = fmt.Errorf("journal_mode is %q, want wal", mode)
			}
		}
		if !isBusy(err) || time.Now().After(deadline) {
			return fmt.Errorf("enable WAL: %w", err)
		}
		time.Sleep(wait)
		if wait < 250*time.Millisecond {
			wait *= 2
		}
	}
}

// isBusy reports SQLITE_BUSY / SQLITE_LOCKED, which the driver may wrap.
func isBusy(err error) bool {
	if err == nil {
		return false
	}
	if errors.Is(err, sqlite3.BUSY) || errors.Is(err, sqlite3.LOCKED) {
		return true
	}
	msg := err.Error()
	return strings.Contains(msg, "database is locked") || strings.Contains(msg, "database table is locked")
}

// migrate re-reads user_version inside an IMMEDIATE transaction so two
// processes opening a fresh file at once do not both run step 1.
func (s *Store) migrate() error {
	tx, err := s.db.Begin()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer tx.Rollback()
	var v int
	if err := tx.QueryRow("PRAGMA user_version").Scan(&v); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if v >= len(migrations) {
		return nil
	}
	for i := v; i < len(migrations); i++ {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(migrations[i]); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("migration %d: %w", i+1, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := tx.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("PRAGMA user_version = %d", len(migrations))); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return tx.Commit()
}

// Close closes the DB (SQLite checkpoints the WAL on last close).
func (s *Store) Close() error { return s.db.Close() }

// cleanupDue reports whether meta.last_cleanup is missing or older than every.
func cleanupDue(q interface {
	QueryRow(string, ...any) *sql.Row
}, now time.Time, every time.Duration) (bool, error) {
	var last string
	err := q.QueryRow("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = 'last_cleanup'").Scan(&last)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return true, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, last)
	return err != nil || now.Sub(t) >= every, nil
}

// CleanupIfDue deletes sources older than retention (all projects) when
// meta.last_cleanup is missing or older than every. Returns sources deleted.
// When not due it only reads: no write lock is taken.
// Port of cleanupStaleSources (ST:1641) gated per spec §3.1.
func (s *Store) CleanupIfDue(now time.Time, retention, every time.Duration) (int, error) {
	if due, err := cleanupDue(s.db, now, every); err != nil || !due {
		return 0, err
	}
	tx, err := s.db.Begin()
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer tx.Rollback()
	// Re-check under the write lock: another process may have just cleaned.
	if due, err := cleanupDue(tx, now, every); err != nil || !due {
		return 0, err
	}
	cutoff := now.Add(-retention).UTC().Format(sqliteTime)
	old := "SELECT id FROM sources WHERE datetime(indexed_at) < datetime(?)"
	if _, err := tx.Exec("DELETE FROM chunks WHERE source_id IN ("+old+")", cutoff); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if _, err := tx.Exec("DELETE FROM chunks_trigram WHERE source_id IN ("+old+")", cutoff); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	res, err := tx.Exec("DELETE FROM sources WHERE datetime(indexed_at) < datetime(?)", cutoff)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	n, err := res.RowsAffected()
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if _, err := tx.Exec("INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES('last_cleanup', ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value",
		now.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return int(n), tx.Commit()
}
```

package store

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// golden is one chunk as produced by the TS ContentStore (store.index /
// indexPlainText / indexJSON + getChunksBySource), captured with tsx.
// sum = first 12 hex chars of sha256(content).
type golden struct {
	title string
	code  bool
	bytes int
	sum   string
}

func checkChunks(t *testing.T, name string, got []Chunk, want []golden) {
	t.Helper()
	if len(got) != len(want) {
		for _, c := range got {
			t.Logf("%s got %q code=%v bytes=%d", name, c.Title, c.HasCode, len(c.Content))
		}
		t.Fatalf("%s: %d chunks, want %d", name, len(got), len(want))
	}
	for i, w := range want {
		h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(got[i].Content))
		g := golden{got[i].Title, got[i].HasCode, len(got[i].Content), hex.EncodeToString(h[:])[:12]}
		if g != w {
			t.Errorf("%s[%d] = %+v, want %+v\ncontent: %.200q", name, i, g, w, got[i].Content)
		}
	}
}

func TestJSStringHelpers(t *testing.T) {

		t.Fatalf("jsTrim = %q", got)
	}
	if jsTrim("\u0085x") != "\u0085x" { // NEL is not JS whitespace
		t.Fatal("jsTrim trimmed U+0085")
	}
	if n := u16len("aé日😀"); n != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("u16len = %d", n)
	}
	if got := u16prefix("ab😀c", 3); got != "ab\ufffd" {
		t.Fatalf("u16prefix cut pair = %q", got)
	}
	if got := u16prefix("ab😀c", 4); got != "ab😀" {
		t.Fatalf("u16prefix = %q", got)
	}
}

func TestChunkMarkdownGolden(t *testing.T) {
	md1 := strings.Join([]string{
		"Intro line", "", "# Title", "Some text", "## Sub A", "```js", "# not a heading", "const x = 1;", "```",
		"---", "More after rule", "### Deep", "deep text", "## Sub B", "b text", "#### Four", "##### five is not heading",
		"***", "# Second", "````", "```", "inner", "````", "tail",
	}, "\n")
	checkChunks(t, "md1", ChunkMarkdown(md1, ChunkMaxBytes), []golden{

		{"Title > Sub A", false, 15, "427234d2217c"},
		{"Title > Sub A > Deep", false, 18, "21a4868f8a0d"},
		{"Title > Sub B", false, 15, "9d5ec916364a"},

	})

	r := strings.Repeat

		"\n\n```\ncode " + r("d", 100) + "\n```\n\n" + r("e", 3000)

	checkChunks(t, "md3", ChunkMarkdown(md3, ChunkMaxBytes), []golden{

		{"Sub", true, 36, "a4dfaa83a444"},

	md4 := "no heading at all\n\n" + r("z", 5000)
	checkChunks(t, "md4", ChunkMarkdown(md4, ChunkMaxBytes), []golden{
		{"Untitled (1)", false, 17, "e35a5aa97721"},
		{"Untitled (2)", false, 5000, "bc9d580ae187"},
	})

	if got := ChunkMarkdown(" \n\n---\n", ChunkMaxBytes); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("blank input gave %d chunks", len(got))
	}
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/store/jsstr.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf16"
)

// JavaScript string semantics the TS code relies on. JS `\s` and trim() use
// this whitespace set (Go's regexp `\s` is ASCII only), and `.length` /
// `.slice()` count UTF-16 code units, not bytes.

// jsWS is the JS `\s` class, for building regexps.
const jsWS = `[\t\n\v\f\r \x{a0}\x{1680}\x{2000}-\x{200a}\x{2028}\x{2029}\x{202f}\x{205f}\x{3000}\x{feff}]`

// jsDot is the JS `.` class without the s flag (excludes line terminators).
const jsDot = `[^\n\r\x{2028}\x{2029}]`

func isJSSpace(r rune) bool {
	switch r {
	case '\t', '\n', '\v', '\f', '\r', ' ', 0xa0, 0x1680, 0x2028, 0x2029, 0x202f, 0x205f, 0x3000, 0xfeff:
		return true
	}
	return r >= 0x2000 && r <= 0x200a
}

// jsTrim is String.prototype.trim.
func jsTrim(s string) string { return strings.TrimFunc(s, isJSSpace) }

// u16len is String.prototype.length.
func u16len(s string) int {
	n := 0
	for _, r := range s {
		n += utf16.RuneLen(r)
	}
	return n
}

// u16prefix is s.slice(0, n). A surrogate pair cut in half becomes U+FFFD,
// as V8 does when the lone surrogate is later written out as UTF-8.
func u16prefix(s string, n int) string {
	u := utf16.Encode([]rune(s))
	if n >= len(u) {
		return s
	}
	return string(utf16.Decode(u[:n]))
}
```

`internal/store/chunk_markdown.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
)

// Chunk is one indexed section. ContentType is Code if HasCode else Prose.
type Chunk struct {
	Title, Content string
	HasCode        bool
}

// ChunkMaxBytes is CHUNK_MAX_BYTES / MAX_CHUNK_BYTES (ST:153).
const ChunkMaxBytes = 4096

var (
	mdRule      = regexp.MustCompile(`^[-_*]{3,}` + jsWS + `*$`)
	mdHeading   = regexp.MustCompile(`^(#{1,4})` + jsWS + `+(` + jsDot + `+)$`)
	mdFence     = regexp.MustCompile("^(`{3,})(" + jsDot + "*)?$")
	mdParagraph = regexp.MustCompile(`\n\n+`)
)

type mdHead struct {
	level int
	text  string
}

// ChunkMarkdown splits Markdown on H1-H4 headings and horizontal rules,
// keeping fenced code blocks whole. Port of #chunkMarkdown (ST:1699-1816).
func ChunkMarkdown(text string, maxBytes int) []Chunk {
	var chunks []Chunk
	lines := strings.Split(text, "\n")
	var stack []mdHead
	var current []string
	currentHeading := ""

	flush := func() {
		joined := jsTrim(strings.Join(current, "\n"))
		if joined == "" {
			return
		}
		title := buildTitle(stack, currentHeading)
		hasCode := false
		for _, l := range current {
			if strings.HasPrefix(l, "```") {
				hasCode = true
				break
			}
		}
		current = nil
		if len(joined) <= maxBytes {
			chunks = append(chunks, Chunk{Title: title, Content: joined, HasCode: hasCode})
			return
		}
		// Oversized: split at paragraph boundaries.
		paragraphs := mdParagraph.Split(joined, -1)
		var acc []string
		part := 1
		flushAcc := func() {
			if len(acc) == 0 {
				return
			}
			p := jsTrim(strings.Join(acc, "\n\n"))
			if p == "" {
				return
			}
			pt := title
			if len(paragraphs) > 1 {
				pt = fmt.Sprintf("%s (%d)", title, part)
			}
			part++
			chunks = append(chunks, Chunk{Title: pt, Content: p, HasCode: strings.Contains(p, "```")})
			acc = nil
		}
		for _, para := range paragraphs {
			acc = append(acc, para)
			if len(strings.Join(acc, "\n\n")) > maxBytes && len(acc) > 1 {
				acc = acc[:len(acc)-1]
				flushAcc()
				acc = []string{para}
			}
		}
		flushAcc()
	}

	for i := 0; i < len(lines); {
		line := lines[i]
		if mdRule.MatchString(line) {
			flush()
			i++
			continue
		}
		if m := mdHeading.FindStringSubmatch(line); m != nil {
			flush()
			level := len(m[1])
			heading := jsTrim(m[2])
			for len(stack) > 0 && stack[len(stack)-1].level >= level {
				stack = stack[:len(stack)-1]
			}
			stack = append(stack, mdHead{level, heading})
			currentHeading = heading
			current = append(current, line)
			i++
			continue
		}
		if m := mdFence.FindStringSubmatch(line); m != nil {
			fence := m[1]
			code := []string{line}
			i++
			for i < len(lines) {
				code = append(code, lines[i])
				if strings.HasPrefix(lines[i], fence) && jsTrim(lines[i]) == fence {
					i++
					break
				}
				i++
			}
			current = append(current, code...)
			continue
		}
		current = append(current, line)
		i++
	}
	flush()
	return chunks
}

// buildTitle: ST:2115-2123.
func buildTitle(stack []mdHead, currentHeading string) string {
	if len(stack) == 0 {
		if currentHeading != "" {
			return currentHeading
		}
		return "Untitled"
	}
	parts := make([]string, len(stack))
	for i, h := range stack {
		parts[i] = h.text
	}
	return strings.Join(parts, " > ")
}
```

package store

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestChunkPlainTextGolden(t *testing.T) {
	r := strings.Repeat
	lines := func(n int, f func(i int) string) string {
		out := make([]string, n)
		for i := range out {
			out[i] = f(i)
		}
		return strings.Join(out, "\n")
	}

	checkChunks(t, "pt1", ChunkPlainText("alpha one\nalpha two\n\nbeta one\n\n  \n\ngamma one\ngamma two", 20), []golden{
		{"alpha one", false, 19, "f5fb195ae410"},
		{"beta one", false, 8, "0b467e2c5890"},
		{"gamma one", false, 19, "a4096bb5bbb0"},
	})
	checkChunks(t, "pt2", ChunkPlainText(lines(45, func(i int) string { return fmt.Sprintf("line %d", i+1) }), 20), []golden{
		{"line 1", false, 150, "92ccbe470b6f"},
		{"line 19", false, 159, "42cb3d357b9d"},
		{"line 37", false, 71, "e51c35f213b9"},
	})
	checkChunks(t, "pt3", ChunkPlainText("short\noutput", 20), []golden{
		{"Output", false, 12, "21d4d13b6bf8"},
	})
	checkChunks(t, "pt4", ChunkPlainText("head\n"+r("x", 5000)+" "+r("y", 5000)+"\ntail", 20), []golden{
		{"Output", false, 4, "9f2e6d33a371"},
		{"Output (2.1)", false, 4096, "a2e659dacb46"},
		{"Output (3.2)", false, 4096, "443c0d8c55e2"},
		{"Output (4.3)", false, 1809, "10af494c33de"},
		{"Output (5)", false, 4, "0c62f876ef1d"},
	})
	p80 := "s1 " + r("p", 77)
	checkChunks(t, "pt5", ChunkPlainText("s1 "+r("p", 4500)+"\n\nsec two\n\nsec three", 20), []golden{
		{p80, false, 4096, "4362514a60d5"},
		{p80 + " (2.2)", false, 407, "04aa9474a367"},
		{"sec two", false, 7, "4f47c669ee8c"},
		{"sec three", false, 9, "c993fc189ef6"},
	})
	pt6 := lines(30, func(i int) string {
		if i == 0 {
			return ""
		}
		return fmt.Sprintf("%s %d", r("w", 300), i)
	})
	checkChunks(t, "pt6", ChunkPlainText(pt6, 20), []golden{
		{"Lines 1-20", false, 3943, "8d343a32cd22"},
		{"Lines 1-20 (2)", false, 1823, "8f47b7d9052d"},
		{r("w", 80), false, 3647, "41f582236db0"},
	})
	checkChunks(t, "pt7", ChunkPlainText(r("日本語テキスト", 400)+"\nend", 20), []golden{
		{"Output", false, 4095, "7692b63703f2"},
		{"Output (2.2)", false, 4095, "7692b63703f2"},
		{"Output (3.3)", false, 210, "2a8ca8bd94e6"},
		{"Output (4)", false, 3, "361e48d0308f"},
	})
	if got := ChunkPlainText(" \n\t\n", 20); got != nil {
		t.Fatalf("blank input gave %v", got)
	}

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/store/chunk_text.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

// ST:160-170.
const (
	minBlankLineSections         = 3
	maxBlankLineSections         = 200
	blankSectionStrategyMaxBytes = 5000
	chunkTitleMaxChars           = 80
	whitespaceBreakRatio         = 0.5
	defaultLinesPerChunk         = 20
	plainOverlap                 = 2
)

var blankLineSplit = regexp.MustCompile(`\n` + jsWS + `*\n`)

// ChunkPlainText splits logs and command output: blank-line sections when the
// output is naturally sectioned, else one "Output" chunk, else windows of
// linesPerChunk lines with a 2-line overlap. Chunks are always prose.
// Port of indexPlainText + #chunkPlainText (ST:1019-1040, ST:1911-1976).
func ChunkPlainText(text string, linesPerChunk int) []Chunk {
	if jsTrim(text) == "" {
		return nil
	}
	if linesPerChunk <= 0 {
		linesPerChunk = defaultLinesPerChunk
	}
	maxBytes := ChunkMaxBytes

	sections := blankLineSplit.Split(text, -1)
	if len(sections) >= minBlankLineSections && len(sections) <= maxBlankLineSections && allUnder(sections, blankSectionStrategyMaxBytes) {
		var out []Chunk
		for i, sec := range sections {
			trimmed := jsTrim(sec)
			if trimmed == "" {
				continue
			}
			first, _, _ := strings.Cut(trimmed, "\n")
			title := u16prefix(first, chunkTitleMaxChars)
			if title == "" {
				title = fmt.Sprintf("Section %d", i+1)
			}
			if len(trimmed) <= maxBytes {
				out = append(out, Chunk{Title: title, Content: trimmed})
				continue
			}
			out = append(out, splitOversizedPlain(strings.Split(trimmed, "\n"), title, maxBytes)...)
		}
		return out
	}

	lines := strings.Split(text, "\n")
	if len(lines) <= linesPerChunk {
		if len(text) <= maxBytes {
			return []Chunk{{Title: "Output", Content: text}}
		}
		return splitOversizedPlain(lines, "Output", maxBytes)
	}

	var out []Chunk
	step := max(linesPerChunk-plainOverlap, 1)
	for i := 0; i < len(lines); i += step {
		slice := lines[i:min(i+linesPerChunk, len(lines))]
		title := u16prefix(jsTrim(slice[0]), chunkTitleMaxChars)
		if title == "" {
			title = fmt.Sprintf("Lines %d-%d", i+1, min(i+len(slice), len(lines)))
		}
		joined := strings.Join(slice, "\n")
		if len(joined) <= maxBytes {
			out = append(out, Chunk{Title: title, Content: joined})
		} else {
			out = append(out, splitOversizedPlain(slice, title, maxBytes)...)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func allUnder(ss []string, n int) bool {
	for _, s := range ss {
		if len(s) >= n {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// byteCappedPrefix: largest prefix of s within maxBytes, never cutting a
// character; always at least one character (ST:1825-1839).
func byteCappedPrefix(s string, maxBytes int) string {
	if len(s) <= maxBytes {
		return s
	}
	n := 0
	for _, r := range s {
		w := utf8.RuneLen(r)
		if n+w > maxBytes {
			break
		}
		n += w
	}
	if n == 0 {
		_, w := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
		return s[:w]
	}
	return s[:n]
}

// splitOversizedPlain: ST:1846-1909. Both part counters advance per piece,
// which gives titles like "Output (2.1)", "Output (3.2)".
func splitOversizedPlain(lines []string, titlePrefix string, maxBytes int) []Chunk {
	var out []Chunk
	var acc []string
	part := 1
	flush := func() {
		if len(acc) == 0 {
			return
		}
		title := titlePrefix
		if part != 1 {
			title = fmt.Sprintf("%s (%d)", titlePrefix, part)
		}
		out = append(out, Chunk{Title: title, Content: strings.Join(acc, "\n")})
		part++
		acc = nil
	}
	for _, line := range lines {
		if len(line) > maxBytes {
			flush()
			remaining := line
			linePart := 1
			for remaining != "" {
				slice := byteCappedPrefix(remaining, maxBytes)
				if len(slice) < len(remaining) {
					bp := max(strings.LastIndex(slice, " "), strings.LastIndex(slice, "\n"))
					if bp >= 0 && float64(u16len(slice[:bp])) > float64(u16len(slice))*whitespaceBreakRatio {
						slice = slice[:bp]
					}
				}
				title := titlePrefix
				if part != 1 || linePart != 1 {
					title = fmt.Sprintf("%s (%d.%d)", titlePrefix, part, linePart)
				}
				out = append(out, Chunk{Title: title, Content: slice})
				remaining = remaining[len(slice):]
				linePart++
				part++
			}
			continue
		}
		candidate := line
		if len(acc) > 0 {
			candidate = strings.Join(acc, "\n") + "\n" + line
		}
		if len(candidate) > maxBytes && len(acc) > 0 {
			flush()
		}
		acc = append(acc, line)
	}
	flush()
	return out
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/store/chunk_json.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"math"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

// jsonObj is a parsed JSON object with JavaScript property order:
// integer-like keys ascending first, then the rest in insertion order.
type jsonObj struct {
	keys []string
	vals map[string]any
}

// Parsed values: nil, bool, float64, string, []any, *jsonObj.

// ChunkJSON walks a JSON document and emits chunks titled by key path
// ("a > b"); arrays are batched under maxBytes and titled by an identity
// field or index range. Port of indexJSON/#walkJSON/#chunkJSONArray
// (ST:1051-1076, ST:1978-2113). Returns an error for invalid or empty JSON;
// the caller then falls back to ChunkPlainText(text, 20), as TS does (also
// when no chunks come back).
func ChunkJSON(text string, maxBytes int) ([]Chunk, error) {
	if jsTrim(text) == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("empty JSON")
	}
	dec := json.NewDecoder(strings.NewReader(text))
	dec.UseNumber()
	v, err := decodeJSON(dec)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if _, err := dec.Token(); err != io.EOF {
		return nil, errors.New("trailing data after JSON value")
	}
	var chunks []Chunk
	walkJSON(v, nil, &chunks, maxBytes)
	return chunks, nil
}

func decodeJSON(dec *json.Decoder) (any, error) {
	tok, err := dec.Token()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	switch t := tok.(type) {
	case json.Delim:
		if t == '[' {
			arr := []any{}
			for dec.More() {
				v, err := decodeJSON(dec)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				arr = append(arr, v)
			}
			_, err := dec.Token()
			return arr, err
		}
		obj := &jsonObj{vals: map[string]any{}}
		for dec.More() {
			kt, err := dec.Token()
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			k := kt.(string)
			v, err := decodeJSON(dec)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if _, dup := obj.vals[k]; !dup {
				obj.keys = append(obj.keys, k)
			}
			obj.vals[k] = v
		}
		if _, err := dec.Token(); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		var ints, rest []string
		for _, k := range obj.keys {
			if isArrayIndex(k) {
				ints = append(ints, k)
			} else {
				rest = append(rest, k)
			}
		}
		slices.SortFunc(ints, func(a, b string) int {
			x, _ := strconv.ParseUint(a, 10, 64)
			y, _ := strconv.ParseUint(b, 10, 64)
			return int(x) - int(y)
		})
		obj.keys = append(ints, rest...)
		return obj, nil
	case json.Number:
		f, err := strconv.ParseFloat(string(t), 64)
		if err != nil && !math.IsInf(f, 0) {
			return nil, err
		}
		return f, nil
	default: // nil, bool, string
		return t, nil
	}
}

// isArrayIndex: canonical uint32 below 2^32-1, which JS orders first.
func isArrayIndex(k string) bool {
	if k == "" || len(k) > 10 || (len(k) > 1 && k[0] == '0') {
		return false
	}
	n, err := strconv.ParseUint(k, 10, 64)
	return err == nil && n < math.MaxUint32
}

func walkJSON(v any, path []string, chunks *[]Chunk, maxBytes int) {
	title := "(root)"
	if len(path) > 0 {
		title = strings.Join(path, " > ")
	}
	serialized := jsonStringify(v, "")
	obj, isObj := v.(*jsonObj)
	if len(serialized) <= maxBytes {
		recurse := false
		if isObj {
			for _, k := range obj.keys {
				if isJSONContainer(obj.vals[k]) {
					recurse = true
					break
				}
			}
		}
		if !recurse {
			*chunks = append(*chunks, Chunk{Title: title, Content: serialized, HasCode: true})
			return
		}
	}
	if isObj {
		if len(obj.keys) == 0 {
			*chunks = append(*chunks, Chunk{Title: title, Content: serialized, HasCode: true})
			return
		}
		for _, k := range obj.keys {
			walkJSON(obj.vals[k], append(slices.Clip(path), k), chunks, maxBytes)
		}
		return
	}
	if arr, ok := v.([]any); ok {
		chunkJSONArray(arr, path, chunks, maxBytes)
		return
	}
	*chunks = append(*chunks, Chunk{Title: title, Content: serialized, HasCode: false})
}

func isJSONContainer(v any) bool {
	switch v.(type) {
	case *jsonObj, []any:
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// chunkJSONArray: ST:2077-2113. JSON.stringify(batch, null, 2) is built from
// items serialized once, so its length is tracked without re-serializing.
func chunkJSONArray(arr []any, path []string, chunks *[]Chunk, maxBytes int) {
	prefix := "(root)"
	if len(path) > 0 {
		prefix = strings.Join(path, " > ")
	}
	idField := findIdentityField(arr)
	var batch []any
	var items []string
	size := 0 // len("[\n" + strings.Join(items, ",\n") + "\n]")
	start := 0
	flush := func(end int) {
		if len(batch) == 0 {
			return
		}
		*chunks = append(*chunks, Chunk{
			Title:   jsonBatchTitle(prefix, start, end, batch, idField),
			Content: "[\n" + strings.Join(items, ",\n") + "\n]",
			HasCode: true,
		})
	}
	for i, v := range arr {
		item := "  " + jsonStringify(v, "  ")
		batch = append(batch, v)
		items = append(items, item)
		if len(items) == 1 {
			size = 4 + len(item)
		} else {
			size += 2 + len(item)
		}
		if size > maxBytes && len(batch) > 1 {
			batch, items = batch[:len(batch)-1], items[:len(items)-1]
			flush(i - 1)
			batch, items = []any{v}, []string{item}
			size = 4 + len(item)
			start = i
		}
	}
	flush(start + len(batch) - 1)
}

// findIdentityField: ST:2035-2048.
func findIdentityField(arr []any) string {
	if len(arr) == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	first, ok := arr[0].(*jsonObj)
	if !ok {
		return ""
	}
	for _, f := range []string{"id", "name", "title", "path", "slug", "key", "label"} {
		switch first.vals[f].(type) {
		case string, float64:
			return f
		}
	}
	return ""
}

// jsonBatchTitle: ST:2050-2075.
func jsonBatchTitle(prefix string, start, end int, batch []any, idField string) string {
	sep := ""
	if prefix != "" {
		sep = prefix + " > "
	}
	if idField == "" {
		if start == end {
			return fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", sep, start)
		}
		return fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d-%d]", sep, start, end)
	}
	id := func(item any) string {
		obj, ok := item.(*jsonObj)
		if !ok {
			return "undefined"
		}
		v, ok := obj.vals[idField]
		if !ok {
			return "undefined"
		}
		return jsString(v)
	}
	if len(batch) == 1 {
		return sep + id(batch[0])
	}
	if len(batch) <= 3 {
		ids := make([]string, len(batch))
		for i, b := range batch {
			ids[i] = id(b)
		}
		return sep + strings.Join(ids, ", ")
	}
	return sep + id(batch[0]) + "…" + id(batch[len(batch)-1])
}

// jsString is String(v) for a parsed JSON value.
func jsString(v any) string {
	switch t := v.(type) {
	case nil:
		return "null"
	case bool:
		return strconv.FormatBool(t)
	case float64:
		return jsNumber(t)
	case string:
		return t
	case []any:
		parts := make([]string, len(t))
		for i, e := range t {
			if e != nil {
				parts[i] = jsString(e)
			}
		}
		return strings.Join(parts, ",")
	default:
		return "[object Object]"
	}
}

// jsonStringify is JSON.stringify(v, null, 2) with every line after the
// first prefixed by indent.
func jsonStringify(v any, indent string) string {
	var b bytes.Buffer
	writeJSON(&b, v, indent)
	return b.String()
}

func writeJSON(b *bytes.Buffer, v any, indent string) {
	switch t := v.(type) {
	case nil:
		b.WriteString("null")
	case bool:
		b.WriteString(strconv.FormatBool(t))
	case float64:
		if math.IsInf(t, 0) || math.IsNaN(t) {
			b.WriteString("null")
		} else {
			b.WriteString(jsNumber(t))
		}
	case string:
		writeJSONString(b, t)
	case []any:
		if len(t) == 0 {
			b.WriteString("[]")
			return
		}
		inner := indent + "  "
		b.WriteString("[\n")
		for i, e := range t {
			if i > 0 {
				b.WriteString(",\n")
			}
			b.WriteString(inner)
			writeJSON(b, e, inner)
		}
		b.WriteString("\n" + indent + "]")
	case *jsonObj:
		if len(t.keys) == 0 {
			b.WriteString("{}")
			return
		}
		inner := indent + "  "
		b.WriteString("{\n")
		for i, k := range t.keys {
			if i > 0 {
				b.WriteString(",\n")
			}
			b.WriteString(inner)
			writeJSONString(b, k)
			b.WriteString(": ")
			writeJSON(b, t.vals[k], inner)
		}
		b.WriteString("\n" + indent + "}")
	}
}

// writeJSONString quotes like JSON.stringify: only `"`, `\` and control
// characters are escaped (no HTML or U+2028 escaping, unlike encoding/json).
func writeJSONString(b *bytes.Buffer, s string) {
	b.WriteByte('"')
	for _, r := range s {
		switch r {
		case '"':
			b.WriteString(`\"`)
		case '\\':
			b.WriteString(`\\`)
		case '\b':
			b.WriteString(`\b`)
		case '\f':
			b.WriteString(`\f`)
		case '\n':
			b.WriteString(`\n`)
		case '\r':
			b.WriteString(`\r`)
		case '\t':
			b.WriteString(`\t`)
		default:
			if r < 0x20 {
				fmt.Fprintf(b, `\u%04x`, r)
			} else {
				b.WriteRune(r)
			}
		}
	}
	b.WriteByte('"')
}

// jsNumber is Number.prototype.toString() for finite values (ECMA-262
// Number::toString, radix 10).
func jsNumber(f float64) string {
	if f == 0 {
		return "0"
	}
	if f < 0 {
		return "-" + jsNumber(-f)
	}
	e := strconv.FormatFloat(f, 'e', -1, 64) // "d.ddde±XX"
	mant, exp, _ := strings.Cut(e, "e")
	digits := strings.Replace(mant, ".", "", 1)
	x, _ := strconv.Atoi(exp)
	k, n := len(digits), x+1
	switch {
	case k <= n && n <= 21:
		return digits + strings.Repeat("0", n-k)
	case 0 < n && n <= 21:
		return digits[:n] + "." + digits[n:]
	case -6 < n && n <= 0:
		return "0." + strings.Repeat("0", -n) + digits
	}
	sign := "+"
	if n-1 < 0 {
		sign = "-"
	}
	ex := strconv.Itoa(abs(n - 1))
	if k == 1 {
		return digits + "e" + sign + ex
	}
	return digits[:1] + "." + digits[1:] + "e" + sign + ex
}

func abs(n int) int {
	if n < 0 {
		return -n
	}
	return n
}
```

// Two Stores (two processes in production) writing the same file at once.
func TestConcurrentStoresSameFile(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "cmgo.db")
	a, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer a.Close()
	b, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer b.Close()

	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	errs := make(chan error, 202)
	for w, st := range []*Store{a, b} {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			for i := range 50 {
				label := fmt.Sprintf("w%d-%d", w, i)
				if _, err := st.Index("p", label, ChunkMarkdown("# "+label+"\nbody text "+label, ChunkMaxBytes)); err != nil {
					errs <- err
				}
				if _, err := st.Index("p", "shared", []Chunk{{Title: "s", Content: fmt.Sprintf("shared %d %d", w, i)}}); err != nil {
					errs <- err
				}
			}
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	close(errs)
	for err := range errs {
		t.Error(err)
	}
	if c, err := a.ChunkCount("p"); err != nil || c != 101 {
		t.Fatalf("ChunkCount = %d, %v", c, err)
	}
	var n int
	b.db.QueryRow("SELECT count(*) FROM sources WHERE label = 'shared'").Scan(&n)
	if n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("shared sources = %d", n)
	}
	b.db.QueryRow("SELECT count(*) FROM chunks").Scan(&n)
	if n != 101 {
		t.Fatalf("chunks rows = %d", n)
	}
}

```go
package store

// stopwords is STOPWORDS (ST:51-65), copied verbatim.
var stopwords = func() map[string]bool {
	m := map[string]bool{}
	for _, w := range []string{
		"the", "and", "for", "are", "but", "not", "you", "all", "can", "had",
		"her", "was", "one", "our", "out", "has", "his", "how", "its", "may",
		"new", "now", "old", "see", "way", "who", "did", "get", "got", "let",
		"say", "she", "too", "use", "will", "with", "this", "that", "from",
		"they", "been", "have", "many", "some", "them", "than", "each", "make",
		"like", "just", "over", "such", "take", "into", "year", "your", "good",
		"could", "would", "about", "which", "their", "there", "other", "after",
		"should", "through", "also", "more", "most", "only", "very", "when",
		"what", "then", "these", "those", "being", "does", "done", "both",
		"same", "still", "while", "where", "here", "were", "much",
		// Common in code/changelogs
		"update", "updates", "updated", "deps", "dev", "tests", "test",
		"add", "added", "fix", "fixed", "run", "running", "using",
	} {
		m[w] = true
	}
	return m
}()

`internal/store/index.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// IndexResult reports one Index call.
type IndexResult struct {
	SourceID                int64
	Label                   string
	TotalChunks, CodeChunks int
	Sections                []Section
}

// Section is one chunk in the "## Indexed Sections" list.
type Section struct {
	Title string
	Bytes int
}

// Source is one row of sources.
type Source struct {
	ID                         int64
	Label                      string
	ChunkCount, CodeChunkCount int
	IndexedAt                  time.Time
}

// optimizeEvery: ContentStore.OPTIMIZE_EVERY (ST:463).
const optimizeEvery = 50

var vocabSplit = regexp.MustCompile(`[^\p{L}\p{N}_-]+`)

// vocabWords: lowercase, split on [^\p{L}\p{N}_-]+, keep words of at least 3
// chars that are not stopwords; unique, in first-seen order (ST:1675-1681).
func vocabWords(text string) []string {
	var out []string
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, w := range vocabSplit.Split(strings.ToLower(text), -1) {
		if u16len(w) >= 3 && !stopwords[w] && !seen[w] {
			seen[w] = true
			out = append(out, w)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// Index replaces the (project, label) source with chunks in one transaction
// and adds their words to the vocabulary. Port of #insertChunks
// (ST:1085-1143). Vocabulary comes from the chunk contents because the raw
// text is not passed in.
func (s *Store) Index(project, label string, chunks []Chunk) (IndexResult, error) {
	res := IndexResult{Label: label, TotalChunks: len(chunks)}
	contents := make([]string, len(chunks))
	for i, c := range chunks {
		if c.HasCode {
			res.CodeChunks++
		}
		res.Sections = append(res.Sections, Section{Title: c.Title, Bytes: len(c.Content)})
		contents[i] = c.Content
	}

	tx, err := s.db.Begin()
	if err != nil {
		return res, err
	}
	defer tx.Rollback()
	for _, q := range []string{
		"DELETE FROM chunks WHERE source_id IN (SELECT id FROM sources WHERE project = ? AND label = ?)",
		"DELETE FROM chunks_trigram WHERE source_id IN (SELECT id FROM sources WHERE project = ? AND label = ?)",
		"DELETE FROM sources WHERE project = ? AND label = ?",
	} {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(q, project, label); err != nil {
			return res, err
		}
	}
	r, err := tx.Exec("INSERT INTO sources (project, label, chunk_count, code_chunk_count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
		project, label, res.TotalChunks, res.CodeChunks)
	if err != nil {
		return res, err
	}
	if res.SourceID, err = r.LastInsertId(); err != nil {
		return res, err
	}
	for _, table := range []string{"chunks", "chunks_trigram"} {
		stmt, err := tx.Prepare("INSERT INTO " + table + " (title, content, source_id, content_type) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)")
		if err != nil {
			return res, err
		}
		for _, c := range chunks {
			ct := Prose
			if c.HasCode {
				ct = Code
			}
			if _, err := stmt.Exec(c.Title, c.Content, res.SourceID, string(ct)); err != nil {
				stmt.Close()
				return res, err
			}
		}
		stmt.Close()
	}
	var added int64
	if words := vocabWords(strings.Join(contents, "\n")); len(words) > 0 {
		stmt, err := tx.Prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO vocabulary (word) VALUES (?)")
		if err != nil {
			return res, err
		}
		for _, w := range words {
			r, err := stmt.Exec(w)
			if err != nil {
				stmt.Close()
				return res, err
			}
			n, _ := r.RowsAffected()
			added += n
		}
		stmt.Close()
	}
	if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
		return res, err
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	if added > 0 { // new words can change fuzzy answers (ST:1694)
		s.fuzzyKeys, s.fuzzyVals = nil, map[string]string{}
	}
	s.indexCalls++
	optimize := s.indexCalls%optimizeEvery == 0
	s.mu.Unlock()
	if optimize { // best effort, like #optimizeFTS (ST:1661)
		s.db.Exec("INSERT INTO chunks(chunks) VALUES('optimize')")
		s.db.Exec("INSERT INTO chunks_trigram(chunks_trigram) VALUES('optimize')")
	}
	return res, nil
}

// SourceByLabel returns the (project, label) source, or nil if missing.
func (s *Store) SourceByLabel(project, label string) (*Source, error) {
	var src Source
	var at string
	err := s.db.QueryRow("SELECT id, label, chunk_count, code_chunk_count, indexed_at FROM sources WHERE project = ? AND label = ?",
		project, label).Scan(&src.ID, &src.Label, &src.ChunkCount, &src.CodeChunkCount, &at)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	src.IndexedAt, err = time.Parse(sqliteTime, at)
	return &src, err
}

// ChunkCount is the number of chunks indexed for project.
func (s *Store) ChunkCount(project string) (int, error) {
	var n int
	err := s.db.QueryRow("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(chunk_count), 0) FROM sources WHERE project = ?", project).Scan(&n)
	return n, err
}
```

```go
package store

import (
	"testing"
)

// hit is a TS searchWithFallback result: source, title, contentType, rank.
type hit struct {
	source, title string
	ct            ContentType
	rank          float64
}

// seedCorpus indexes the corpus used by the TS probe (store.index for the
// docs, store.indexPlainText for the log).
func seedCorpus(t *testing.T, s *Store, project string) {
	t.Helper()
	docs := [][2]string{
		{"docA", "# Connection Pool\nThe connection pool manages database connections for the app.\n## Timeout\nSet the pool timeout to 30s so idle connection slots close."},
		{"docB", "# HTTP Server\nThe server handles connection upgrades and websocket frames.\n```go\nfunc handleConnection(c net.Conn) { pool.Get() }\n```"},
		{"docC", "# Notes\nRandom notes about caching layers and pool sizing.\n## Caching\nThe caching layer sits in front of the database pool."},
		{"docD", "# Deploy\nKubernetes deployment rollout strategy.\n## Rollback\nRollback the deployment when the connection check fails."},
	}
	for _, d := range docs {
		if _, err := s.Index(project, d[0], ChunkMarkdown(d[1], ChunkMaxBytes)); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	log := "error: connection refused\nretrying connection pool\n\nwarn: pool exhausted\n\nfatal: giving up"
	if _, err := s.Index(project, "log:run", ChunkPlainText(log, 20)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func checkHits(t *testing.T, name string, got []SearchResult, want []hit) {
	t.Helper()
	if len(got) != len(want) {
		for _, r := range got {
			t.Logf("%s got %q %q %s %v", name, r.Source, r.Title, r.ContentType, r.Rank)
		}
		t.Fatalf("%s: %d hits, want %d", name, len(got), len(want))
	}
	for i, w := range want {
		g := hit{got[i].Source, got[i].Title, got[i].ContentType, got[i].Rank}
		if g != w {
			t.Errorf("%s[%d] = %+v, want %+v", name, i, g, w)
		}
	}
}

func TestSanitizeGolden(t *testing.T) {
	// Expected values from TS sanitizeQuery(q, "OR") / sanitizeTrigramQuery(q, "OR").
	cases := []struct{ in, porter, trigram string }{
		{"connection pool", "\"connection\" OR \"pool\"", "\"connection\" OR \"pool\""},
		{"the AND or", "\"the\"", "\"the\" OR \"AND\""},
		{"Error error ERROR", "\"Error\"", "\"Error\""},
		{"foo:bar (baz) \"quoted\" *star*", "\"foo\" OR \"bar\" OR \"baz\" OR \"quoted\" OR \"star\"", "\"foobar\" OR \"baz\" OR \"quoted\" OR \"star\""},
		{"a b", "\"a\" OR \"b\"", ""},
		{"x", "\"x\"", ""},
		{"update the tests", "\"update\" OR \"the\" OR \"tests\"", "\"update\" OR \"the\" OR \"tests\""},
		{"  near NEAR not  ", "\"\"", "\"near\""},
		{"ab abc abcd", "\"ab\" OR \"abc\" OR \"abcd\"", "\"abc\" OR \"abcd\""},
		{"café's naïve", "\"café\" OR \"s\" OR \"naïve\"", "\"cafés\" OR \"naïve\""},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		if got := SanitizeQuery(c.in); got != c.porter {
			t.Errorf("SanitizeQuery(%q) = %q, want %q", c.in, got, c.porter)
		}
		if got := SanitizeTrigramQuery(c.in); got != c.trigram {
			t.Errorf("SanitizeTrigramQuery(%q) = %q, want %q", c.in, got, c.trigram)
		}
	}
}

func TestRRFRerankGolden(t *testing.T) {
	s := openTest(t)
	seedCorpus(t, s, "p")
	seedCorpus(t, s, "other") // must not leak into project p
	search := func(q string, o SearchOpts) []SearchResult {
		t.Helper()
		o.Project = "p"
		if o.Limit == 0 {
			o.Limit = 3
		}
		res, err := s.rrfSearch(q, o.Limit, o)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return rerank(res, q)
	}
	// Expected values from TS store.searchWithFallback (relevance path).
	checkHits(t, "connection pool", search("connection pool", SearchOpts{}), []hit{
		{"docA", "Connection Pool", Prose, -0.03278688524590164},
		{"log:run", "error: connection refused", Prose, -0.031746031746031744},
		{"docA", "Connection Pool > Timeout", Prose, -0.03225806451612903},
	})
	checkHits(t, "websocket", search("websocket", SearchOpts{}), []hit{
		{"docB", "HTTP Server", Code, -0.03278688524590164},
	})
	checkHits(t, "handleConnection", search("handleConnection", SearchOpts{}), []hit{
		{"docB", "HTTP Server", Code, -0.03278688524590164},
	})
	checkHits(t, "pool timeout", search("pool timeout", SearchOpts{}), []hit{
		{"docA", "Connection Pool > Timeout", Prose, -0.03278688524590164},
		{"log:run", "warn: pool exhausted", Prose, -0.03225806451612903},
		{"docA", "Connection Pool", Prose, -0.031746031746031744},
	})
	checkHits(t, "caching layer", search("caching layer", SearchOpts{}), []hit{

		{"docC", "Notes", Prose, -0.03225806451612903},

	checkHits(t, "the pool", search("the pool", SearchOpts{}), []hit{

		{"docA", "Connection Pool", Prose, -0.03225806451612903},
		{"docA", "Connection Pool > Timeout", Prose, -0.031746031746031744},

		{"docD", "Deploy", Prose, -0.03225806451612903},

		{"docB", "HTTP Server", Code, -0.03278688524590164},
	})
	checkHits(t, "code only", search("connection", SearchOpts{ContentType: Code}), []hit{
		{"docB", "HTTP Server", Code, -0.03278688524590164},
	})
	checkHits(t, "pool limit 5", search("pool", SearchOpts{Limit: 5}), []hit{
		{"log:run", "warn: pool exhausted", Prose, -0.03278688524590164},
		{"docA", "Connection Pool", Prose, -0.03225806451612903},
		{"docA", "Connection Pool > Timeout", Prose, -0.031746031746031744},
		{"log:run", "error: connection refused", Prose, -0.031009615384615385},
		{"docC", "Notes", Prose, -0.031009615384615385},
	})
	if got := search("zzzz qqqq", SearchOpts{}); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("zzzz qqqq: %d hits", len(got))
	}
	// LIKE metacharacters are literal: "doc_" matches nothing.
	if got := search("connection", SearchOpts{Source: "doc_"}); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("doc_ matched %d", len(got))
	}
	// Empty porter query ("") must not error.
	if _, err := s.rrfSearch("near not", 3, SearchOpts{Project: "p"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	res := search("websocket frames", SearchOpts{Limit: 1})
	want := "# HTTP Server\nThe server handles connection upgrades and \x02websocket\x03 \x02frames\x03.\n```go\nfunc handleConnection(c net.Conn) { pool.Get() }\n```"
	if len(res) != 1 || res[0].Highlighted != want {
		t.Fatalf("highlighted = %+v", res)
	}
	if res[0].IndexedAt.IsZero() {
		t.Fatal("IndexedAt not set")
	}
}

func TestRerankHelpers(t *testing.T) {
	if got := findMinSpan([][]int{{0, 50}, {10, 60}, {55}}); got != 10 {
		t.Fatalf("findMinSpan = %d", got)
	}
	// "foo foo bar": the single "bar" pairs with one "foo" only.
	if got := countAdjacentPairs([][]int{{0, 4}, {8}}, []string{"foo", "bar"}, 30); got != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("countAdjacentPairs = %d", got)
	}
}

`internal/store/search.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"cmp"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"time"
	"unicode/utf16"
)

// SearchOpts scopes a search. Project is always applied.
type SearchOpts struct {
	Project     string
	Source      string      // "" = all
	SourceExact bool        // true: label = ?, false: label LIKE %x% ESCAPE '\'
	ContentType ContentType // "" = any
	Limit       int
	Mode        string // "relevance" | "timeline"
}

// SearchResult is one hit.
type SearchResult struct {
	Title, Content, Source string
	ContentType            ContentType
	Highlighted            string // content with \x02/\x03 markers, "" if none
	Rank                   float64
	IndexedAt              time.Time
}

// rrfK: standard RRF constant (ST:1304).
const rrfK = 60

var (
	porterStrip  = regexp.MustCompile(`['"(){}\[\]*:^~]`)
	trigramStrip = regexp.MustCompile(`["'(){}\[\]*:^~]`)
)

func jsFields(s string) []string { return strings.FieldsFunc(s, isJSSpace) }

// dedupeTokens keeps the first spelling of case-insensitive duplicates (ST:77).
func dedupeTokens(tokens []string) []string {
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	var out []string
	for _, t := range tokens {
		if k := strings.ToLower(t); !seen[k] {
			seen[k] = true
			out = append(out, t)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// quoteOr drops stopwords (unless all are stopwords) and joins quoted terms
// with " OR ".
func quoteOr(words []string) string {
	var meaningful []string
	for _, w := range words {
		if !stopwords[strings.ToLower(w)] {
			meaningful = append(meaningful, w)
		}
	}
	if len(meaningful) == 0 {
		meaningful = words
	}
	for i, w := range meaningful {
		meaningful[i] = `"` + w + `"`
	}
	return strings.Join(meaningful, " OR ")
}

// SanitizeQuery builds the porter MATCH expression, OR mode (ST:90-111).
func SanitizeQuery(q string) string {
	var words []string
	for _, w := range jsFields(porterStrip.ReplaceAllString(q, " ")) {
		switch strings.ToUpper(w) {
		case "AND", "OR", "NOT", "NEAR":
			continue
		}
		words = append(words, w)
	}
	words = dedupeTokens(words)
	if len(words) == 0 {
		return `""`
	}
	return quoteOr(words)
}

// SanitizeTrigramQuery builds the trigram MATCH expression, OR mode; "" means
// skip the trigram search (ST:113-125).
func SanitizeTrigramQuery(q string) string {
	cleaned := jsTrim(trigramStrip.ReplaceAllString(q, ""))
	if u16len(cleaned) < 3 {
		return ""
	}
	var words []string
	for _, w := range jsFields(cleaned) {
		if u16len(w) >= 3 {
			words = append(words, w)
		}
	}
	words = dedupeTokens(words)
	if len(words) == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	return quoteOr(words)
}

// likeParam escapes LIKE metacharacters for ESCAPE '\' (ST:1160-1173).
func likeParam(s string) string {
	r := strings.NewReplacer(`\`, `\\`, `%`, `\%`, `_`, `\_`)
	return "%" + r.Replace(s) + "%"
}

// scopeSQL is the shared WHERE tail and its args (after the MATCH arg).
func scopeSQL(table string, o SearchOpts) (string, []any) {
	where := " AND sources.project = ?"
	args := []any{o.Project}
	if o.Source != "" {
		if o.SourceExact {
			where += " AND sources.label = ?"
			args = append(args, o.Source)
		} else {
			where += ` AND sources.label LIKE ? ESCAPE '\'`
			args = append(args, likeParam(o.Source))
		}
	}
	if o.ContentType != "" {
		where += " AND " + table + ".content_type = ?"
		args = append(args, string(o.ContentType))
	}
	return where, args
}

// ftsSearch runs one FTS table query (ST:638-831 plus the project filter).
func (s *Store) ftsSearch(table, match string, limit int, o SearchOpts) ([]SearchResult, error) {
	where, args := scopeSQL(table, o)
	q := "SELECT " + table + ".title, " + table + ".content, " + table + ".content_type, sources.label, " +
		"bm25(" + table + ", 5.0, 1.0) AS rank, highlight(" + table + ", 1, char(2), char(3)), sources.indexed_at " +
		"FROM " + table + " JOIN sources ON sources.id = " + table + ".source_id " +
		"WHERE " + table + " MATCH ?" + where + " ORDER BY rank LIMIT ?"
	args = append(append([]any{match}, args...), limit)
	rows, err := s.db.Query(q, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	var out []SearchResult
	for rows.Next() {
		var r SearchResult
		var ct, at string
		if err := rows.Scan(&r.Title, &r.Content, &ct, &r.Source, &r.Rank, &r.Highlighted, &at); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		r.ContentType = ContentType(ct)
		if !strings.Contains(r.Highlighted, "\x02") {
			r.Highlighted = ""
		}
		r.IndexedAt, _ = time.Parse(sqliteTime, at)
		out = append(out, r)
	}
	return out, rows.Err()
}

// rrfSearch fuses porter and trigram hits by reciprocal rank, keyed by
// source::title; Rank = -score (ST:1297-1337).
func (s *Store) rrfSearch(query string, limit int, o SearchOpts) ([]SearchResult, error) {
	fetch := max(limit*2, 10)
	porter, err := s.ftsSearch("chunks", SanitizeQuery(query), fetch, o)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var trigram []SearchResult
	if tq := SanitizeTrigramQuery(query); tq != "" {
		if trigram, err = s.ftsSearch("chunks_trigram", tq, fetch, o); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	type scored struct {
		r     SearchResult
		score float64
	}
	var order []*scored // insertion order: porter first, then trigram
	byKey := map[string]*scored{}
	for _, list := range [][]SearchResult{porter, trigram} {
		for i, r := range list {
			k := r.Source + "::" + r.Title
			if e := byKey[k]; e != nil {
				e.score += 1 / float64(rrfK+i+1)
				continue
			}
			e := &scored{r, 1 / float64(rrfK+i+1)}
			byKey[k] = e
			order = append(order, e)
		}
	}
	slices.SortStableFunc(order, func(a, b *scored) int { return cmp.Compare(b.score, a.score) })
	out := make([]SearchResult, 0, min(limit, len(order)))
	for _, e := range order[:min(limit, len(order))] {
		e.r.Rank = -e.score
		out = append(out, e.r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// rerank applies title, proximity and phrase boosts; ties keep RRF order
// (ST:1341-1389). Offsets are UTF-16 code units, as in JS.
func rerank(results []SearchResult, query string) []SearchResult {
	var all, filtered []string
	for _, w := range jsFields(strings.ToLower(query)) {
		if u16len(w) >= 2 {
			all = append(all, w)
			if !stopwords[w] {
				filtered = append(filtered, w)
			}
		}
	}
	terms := filtered
	if len(terms) == 0 {
		terms = all
	}
	type boosted struct {
		r     SearchResult
		boost float64
	}
	bs := make([]boosted, len(results))
	for i, r := range results {
		titleLower := strings.ToLower(r.Title)
		hits := 0
		for _, t := range terms {
			if strings.Contains(titleLower, t) {
				hits++
			}
		}
		weight := 0.3
		if r.ContentType == Code {
			weight = 0.6
		}
		titleBoost := 0.0
		if hits > 0 {
			titleBoost = weight * (float64(hits) / float64(len(terms)))
		}
		proximity, phrase := 0.0, 0.0
		if len(terms) >= 2 {
			content := utf16.Encode([]rune(strings.ToLower(r.Content)))
			positions := make([][]int, len(terms))
			missing := false
			for j, t := range terms {
				positions[j] = findAllPositions(content, utf16.Encode([]rune(t)))
				missing = missing || len(positions[j]) == 0
			}
			if !missing {
				minSpan := findMinSpan(positions)
				proximity = 1 / (1 + float64(minSpan)/float64(max(len(content), 1)))
				phrase = 0.5 * min(1, float64(countAdjacentPairs(positions, terms, 30))/4)
			}
		}
		bs[i] = boosted{r, titleBoost + proximity + phrase}
	}
	slices.SortStableFunc(bs, func(a, b boosted) int {
		if c := cmp.Compare(b.boost, a.boost); c != 0 {
			return c
		}
		return cmp.Compare(a.r.Rank, b.r.Rank)
	})
	out := make([]SearchResult, len(bs))
	for i, b := range bs {
		out[i] = b.r
	}
	return out
}

// findAllPositions: overlapping indexOf matches (ST:319-327).
func findAllPositions(text, term []uint16) []int {
	var out []int
	for i := 0; i+len(term) <= len(text); i++ {
		if slices.Equal(text[i:i+len(term)], term) {
			out = append(out, i)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// countAdjacentPairs: ST:340-364.
func countAdjacentPairs(lists [][]int, terms []string, gap int) int {
	if len(lists) < 2 || len(terms) < 2 {
		return 0
	}
	total := 0
	for i := 0; i < min(len(lists), len(terms))-1; i++ {
		left, right := lists[i], lists[i+1]
		leftLen := u16len(terms[i])
		j := 0
		for _, p := range left {
			minStart := p + leftLen
			for j < len(right) && right[j] < minStart {
				j++
			}
			if j < len(right) && right[j] <= minStart+gap {
				total++
				j++
			}
		}
	}
	return total
}

// findMinSpan: smallest window holding one position from each list
// (ST:370-402). Lists are non-empty and sorted.
func findMinSpan(lists [][]int) int {
	if len(lists) == 1 {
		return 0
	}
	ptrs := make([]int, len(lists))
	best := -1
	for {
		curMin, curMax, minIdx := lists[0][ptrs[0]], lists[0][ptrs[0]], 0
		for i := 1; i < len(lists); i++ {
			v := lists[i][ptrs[i]]
			if v < curMin {
				curMin, minIdx = v, i
			}
			if v > curMax {
				curMax = v
			}
		}
		if span := curMax - curMin; best < 0 || span < best {
			best = span
		}
		ptrs[minIdx]++
		if ptrs[minIdx] >= len(lists[minIdx]) {
			return best
		}
	}
}

		t.Fatalf("non-matching timeline = %+v, %v", res, err)

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/store/fallback.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf16"
)

// fuzzyCacheSize: ContentStore.FUZZY_CACHE_SIZE (ST:471).
const fuzzyCacheSize = 256

// defaultSearchLimit: searchWithFallback's limit default (ST:1395).
const defaultSearchLimit = 3

// SearchWithFallback is the ctx_search entry point: RRF over porter +
// trigram, proximity rerank, then one fuzzy-corrected retry when nothing
// matched (ST:1393-1442). Timeline mode takes those same results and
// stable-sorts them oldest first, as searchAllSources does for the content
// store (src/search/unified.ts:104-187).
func (s *Store) SearchWithFallback(query string, o SearchOpts) ([]SearchResult, error) {
	if o.Limit <= 0 {
		o.Limit = defaultSearchLimit
	}
	res, err := s.relevance(query, o)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if o.Mode == "timeline" {
		slices.SortStableFunc(res, func(a, b SearchResult) int { return a.IndexedAt.Compare(b.IndexedAt) })
	}
	return res[:min(len(res), o.Limit)], nil
}

func (s *Store) relevance(query string, o SearchOpts) ([]SearchResult, error) {
	res, err := s.rrfSearch(query, o.Limit, o)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(res) > 0 {
		return rerank(res, query), nil
	}

	var words, corrected []string
	for _, w := range jsFields(strings.ToLower(jsTrim(query))) {
		if u16len(w) >= 3 && !stopwords[w] {
			words = append(words, w)
		}
	}
	for _, w := range words {
		c, err := s.fuzzyCorrect(w)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if c == "" {
			c = w
		}
		corrected = append(corrected, c)
	}
	cq := strings.Join(corrected, " ")
	if cq == strings.Join(words, " ") {
		return nil, nil
	}
	res, err = s.rrfSearch(cq, o.Limit, o)
	if err != nil || len(res) == 0 {
		return nil, err
	}
	return rerank(res, cq), nil
}

// fuzzyCorrect returns the closest vocabulary word within the edit-distance
// budget, or "" for none / exact match. LRU-cached (ST:1248-1293).
func (s *Store) fuzzyCorrect(query string) (string, error) {
	word := strings.ToLower(jsTrim(query))
	n := u16len(word)
	if n < 3 {
		return "", nil
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	if v, ok := s.fuzzyVals[word]; ok {
		i := slices.Index(s.fuzzyKeys, word)
		s.fuzzyKeys = append(slices.Delete(s.fuzzyKeys, i, i+1), word)
		s.mu.Unlock()
		return v, nil
	}
	s.mu.Unlock()

	maxDist := maxEditDistance(n)
	rows, err := s.db.Query("SELECT word FROM vocabulary WHERE length(word) BETWEEN ? AND ?", n-maxDist, n+maxDist)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	var candidates []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var c string
		if err := rows.Scan(&c); err != nil {
			rows.Close()
			return "", err
		}
		candidates = append(candidates, c)
	}
	rows.Close()
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	best, bestDist, exact := "", maxDist+1, false
	w16 := utf16.Encode([]rune(word))
	for _, c := range candidates {
		if c == word {
			exact = true
			break
		}
		if d := levenshtein(w16, utf16.Encode([]rune(c))); d < bestDist {
			best, bestDist = c, d
		}
	}
	result := ""
	if !exact && bestDist <= maxDist {
		result = best
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	if _, ok := s.fuzzyVals[word]; !ok {
		if len(s.fuzzyKeys) >= fuzzyCacheSize {
			delete(s.fuzzyVals, s.fuzzyKeys[0])
			s.fuzzyKeys = s.fuzzyKeys[1:]
		}
		s.fuzzyKeys = append(s.fuzzyKeys, word)
	}
	s.fuzzyVals[word] = result
	s.mu.Unlock()
	return result, nil
}

// maxEditDistance: ST:144-148.
func maxEditDistance(n int) int {
	switch {
	case n <= 4:
		return 1
	case n <= 12:
		return 2
	}
	return 3
}

// levenshtein over UTF-16 code units (ST:127-142).
func levenshtein(a, b []uint16) int {
	if len(a) == 0 {
		return len(b)
	}
	if len(b) == 0 {
		return len(a)
	}
	prev := make([]int, len(b)+1)
	for i := range prev {
		prev[i] = i
	}
	for i := 1; i <= len(a); i++ {
		curr := make([]int, len(b)+1)
		curr[0] = i
		for j := 1; j <= len(b); j++ {
			if a[i-1] == b[j-1] {
				curr[j] = prev[j-1]
			} else {
				curr[j] = 1 + min(prev[j], curr[j-1], prev[j-1])
			}
		}
		prev = curr
	}
	return prev[len(b)]
}
```

```go
package store

import (
	"cmp"
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"math"
	"slices"
	"strings"
)

// DistinctiveTerms returns up to maxTerms words that appear in 2..max(3, 40%)
// chunks of the source, ranked by IDF + length + identifier bonus; nil for
// sources with fewer than 3 chunks. Port of getDistinctiveTerms (ST:1573-1617).
func (s *Store) DistinctiveTerms(sourceID int64, maxTerms int) ([]string, error) {
	var total int
	err := s.db.QueryRow("SELECT chunk_count FROM sources WHERE id = ?", sourceID).Scan(&total)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) || (err == nil && total < 3) {
		return nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	maxApp := int(math.Max(3, math.Ceil(float64(total)*0.4)))

	rows, err := s.db.Query("SELECT content FROM chunks WHERE source_id = ? ORDER BY rowid", sourceID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	var order []string // first-seen order, like a JS Map
	freq := map[string]int{}
	for rows.Next() {
		var content string
		if err := rows.Scan(&content); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		for _, w := range vocabWords(content) {
			if freq[w] == 0 {
				order = append(order, w)
			}
			freq[w]++
		}
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	type scored struct {
		word  string
		score float64
	}
	var list []scored
	for _, w := range order {
		n := freq[w]
		if n < 2 || n > maxApp {
			continue
		}
		bonus := 0.0
		if strings.Contains(w, "_") {
			bonus = 1.5
		} else if u16len(w) >= 12 {
			bonus = 0.8
		}
		list = append(list, scored{w, math.Log(float64(total)/float64(n)) + math.Min(float64(u16len(w))/20, 0.5) + bonus})
	}
	slices.SortStableFunc(list, func(a, b scored) int { return cmp.Compare(b.score, a.score) })
	out := []string{}
	for _, e := range list[:min(maxTerms, len(list))] {
		out = append(out, e.word)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

`internal/store/snippet.go`:

```go
package store

import (
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf16"
)

// snippetWindow: chars kept on each side of a match (S:1341).
const snippetWindow = 300

// ExtractSnippet returns content whole when it fits in maxLen, else ±300-char
// windows around matches joined by "\n\n" with "…" at cut edges. Matches come
// from the \x02/\x03 markers in highlighted, else from indexOf on query terms
// longer than 2 chars. Port of extractSnippet (S:1270-1367); offsets and
// lengths are UTF-16 code units, as in JS.
func ExtractSnippet(content, query string, maxLen int, highlighted string) string {
	c := utf16.Encode([]rune(content))
	if len(c) <= maxLen {
		return content
	}
	var positions []int
	if highlighted != "" {
		clean := 0
		h := utf16.Encode([]rune(highlighted))
		for i := 0; i < len(h); {
			if h[i] != 0x02 {
				clean++
				i++
				continue
			}
			positions = append(positions, clean)
			i++
			for i < len(h) && h[i] != 0x03 {
				clean++
				i++
			}
			if i < len(h) {
				i++
			}
		}
	}
	if len(positions) == 0 {
		lower := utf16.Encode([]rune(strings.ToLower(content)))
		for _, t := range jsFields(strings.ToLower(query)) {
			if u16len(t) > 2 {
				positions = append(positions, findAllPositions(lower, utf16.Encode([]rune(t)))...)
			}
		}
	}
	if len(positions) == 0 {
		return string(utf16.Decode(c[:maxLen])) + "\n…"
	}

	slices.Sort(positions)
	var windows [][2]int
	for _, p := range positions {
		start, end := max(0, p-snippetWindow), min(len(c), p+snippetWindow)
		if n := len(windows); n > 0 && start <= windows[n-1][1] {
			windows[n-1][1] = end
		} else {
			windows = append(windows, [2]int{start, end})
		}
	}
	var parts []string
	total := 0
	for _, w := range windows {
		if total >= maxLen {
			break
		}
		part := c[w[0]:min(w[1], w[0]+maxLen-total)]
		s := string(utf16.Decode(part))
		if w[0] > 0 {
			s = "…" + s
		}
		if w[1] < len(c) {
			s += "…"
		}
		parts = append(parts, s)
		total += len(part)
	}
	return strings.Join(parts, "\n\n")
}
```
