```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"
	"unicode/utf16"

	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/executor"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/security"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/store"
	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
)

const (
	commandEchoMax   = 500       // S:1477
	batchSnippetMax  = 3000      // S:1398
	batchOutputCap   = 80 * 1024 // S:1375
	batchSearchLimit = 3         // S:1393
	batchTermsMax    = 40        // ST:1573 getDistinctiveTerms default
)

const batchDescription = "Run multiple commands in ONE call. Every command's output is auto-indexed into the knowledge base; if you also pass `queries`, the matching sections come back in the same round trip so a follow-up search call is not needed.\n" +
	"\n" +
	"Concurrency parallelizes the FETCH phase (run-the-commands). The DERIVATION phase — turning raw output into an answer — still belongs in code: add a processing command that consumes the indexed output and prints only the answer, so the raw bytes never enter your conversation (Think-in-Code, same principle as the sandbox tool).\n" +
	"\n" +
	"WHEN:\n" +
	"  - You have 3+ related commands you would otherwise run sequentially (multi-issue lookups, git log + git diff + git blame, multi-file reads, multi-region cloud queries)\n" +
	"  - You want to gather AND query in one round trip — pass `queries` so the matching sections come back inline\n" +
	"  - You want to parallelize I/O-bound work — pass `concurrency` 2-8 (network calls, gh CLI, cloud APIs, multi-repo git reads)\n" +
	"  - The combined output is large enough that piping it through ctx_search later would itself be expensive — let auto-index + inline queries do both in one shot\n" +
	"\n" +
	"WHEN NOT:\n" +
	"  - Single command with no follow-up query — run it in the sandbox tool directly\n" +
	"  - CPU-bound or stateful commands — keep concurrency at 1 (npm test, build, lint, port-binding servers, lock-file holders, anything that races on the same resource)\n" +
	"\n" +
	"RETURNS:\n" +
	"  Auto-indexed section list per command label, plus top matches per query (when `queries` is passed). Raw output is NOT echoed in full — only the matched windows. Concurrency>1 switches each command to its own per-command timeout (no shared budget); concurrency=1 preserves the legacy shared-budget cascading-skip-on-timeout path. Use 4-8 for I/O-bound batches; keep at 1 for CPU work or shared-state commands; lower the value when target hosts enforce per-IP rate limits.\n" +
	"\n" +
	"EXAMPLE: ctx_batch_execute(\n" +
	"  commands: [\n" +
	"    {label: \"issue 1\", command: \"gh issue view 1\"},\n" +
	"    {label: \"issue 2\", command: \"gh issue view 2\"},\n" +
	"    {label: \"summarize\", command: \"echo done\"}\n" +
	"  ],\n" +
	"  queries: [\"root cause\", \"proposed fix\"],\n" +
	"  concurrency: 2\n" +
	")"

// batchSchema mirrors the zod schema at S:3715-3776; coerced fields also accept strings.
const batchSchema = `{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "commands": {
      "type": ["array", "string"],
      "minItems": 1,
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "label": {"type": "string", "description": "Section header for this command's output (e.g., 'README', 'Package.json', 'Source Tree')"},
          "command": {"type": "string", "description": "Shell command to execute"}
        },
        "required": ["label", "command"]
      },
      "description": "Commands to execute as a batch. Output is labeled with the section header. Default order is sequential; pass concurrency>1 to run in parallel (output stays in input order)."
    },
    "queries": {
      "type": ["array", "string"],
      "minItems": 1,
      "items": {"type": "string"},
      "description": "Search queries to extract information from indexed output. Use 5-8 comprehensive queries. Each returns top 5 matching sections with full content. This is your ONLY chance — put ALL your questions here. No follow-up calls needed."
    },
    "timeout": {
      "type": ["number", "string"],
      "description": "Max execution time in ms. When omitted, no server-side timer fires — the MCP host's RPC timeout governs. With concurrency=1, the value (when set) is a shared budget across commands; with concurrency>1, it is applied per-command."
    },
    "concurrency": {
      "type": ["integer", "string"],
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 8,
      "default": 1,
      "description": "Max commands to run in parallel (1-8, default: 1). Use 4-8 for I/O-bound batches (network, gh, curl, multi-repo git reads). Keep at 1 for CPU-bound (npm test, build, lint) or stateful commands (ports, locks). >1 switches to per-command timeouts (no shared budget) and individual ` + "`(timed out)`" + ` blocks instead of cascading skip."
    },
    "cwd": {"type": "string", "description": "Optional working directory for all shell commands in this batch."},
    "query_scope": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": ["batch", "global"],
      "default": "batch",
      "description": "Scope for ` + "`queries`" + ` (default: ` + "`batch`" + `). ` + "`batch`" + ` searches ONLY the chunks produced by this batch's commands — useful when you want answers about the just-fetched output. ` + "`global`" + ` searches the entire persistent index (same scope as ctx_search) — useful when you want the batch commands to enrich context and the queries to also surface related prior knowledge in one round trip."
    }
  },
  "required": ["commands", "queries"]
}`

func registerBatch(s *mcp.Server, d *Deps) {
	t := true
	s.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
		Name:        "ctx_batch_execute",
		Title:       "Batch Execute & Search",
		Description: batchDescription,
		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(batchSchema),
		Annotations: &mcp.ToolAnnotations{DestructiveHint: &t, OpenWorldHint: &t},
	}, func(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
		return batchExecute(ctx, d, req.Params.Arguments), nil
	})
}

// batchArgs is the validated ctx_batch_execute input.
type batchArgs struct {
	commands    []Command
	queries     []string
	timeoutMs   float64
	hasTimeout  bool
	concurrency int
	cwd         string
	scope       string
}

func parseBatchArgs(raw json.RawMessage) (batchArgs, error) {
	var b batchArgs
	a, err := ParseArgs(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return b, err
	}
	var ok bool
	if b.commands, ok, err = a.Commands("commands"); err != nil {
		return b, err
	} else if !ok || len(b.commands) == 0 {
		return b, errors.New("commands: expected a non-empty array")
	}
	if b.queries, ok, err = a.Strings("queries"); err != nil {
		return b, err
	} else if !ok || len(b.queries) == 0 {
		return b, errors.New("queries: expected a non-empty array")
	}
	if b.timeoutMs, b.hasTimeout, err = a.Number("timeout"); err != nil {
		return b, err
	}
	b.concurrency = 1
	if c, ok, err := a.Number("concurrency"); err != nil {
		return b, err
	} else if ok {
		if c != math.Trunc(c) || c < 1 || c > 8 {
			return b, errors.New("concurrency: expected an integer from 1 to 8")
		}
		b.concurrency = int(c)
	}
	if b.cwd, _, err = a.String("cwd"); err != nil {
		return b, err
	}
	b.scope = "batch"
	if sc, ok, err := a.String("query_scope"); err != nil {
		return b, err
	} else if ok {
		if sc != "batch" && sc != "global" {
			return b, errors.New(`query_scope: expected "batch" or "global"`)
		}
		b.scope = sc
	}
	return b, nil
}

// batchExecute is the ctx_batch_execute handler (S:3778-3892).
func batchExecute(ctx context.Context, d *Deps, raw json.RawMessage) *mcp.CallToolResult {
	b, err := parseBatchArgs(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return ArgError(err)
	}
	for _, c := range b.commands {
		if blocked, pattern := d.Policy.CheckCommand(c.Command); blocked {
			return TextResult(security.BlockedMessage(pattern), true)
		}
	}

	outputs, err := runBatchCommands(ctx, d.Exec, b)
	if err != nil {
		return ErrorResult("Batch execution error: %v", err)
	}
	stdout := strings.Join(outputs, "\n")
	totalLines := strings.Count(stdout, "\n") + 1

	labels := make([]string, len(b.commands))
	for i, c := range b.commands {
		labels[i] = c.Label
	}
	source := "batch:" + jsPrefix(strings.Join(labels, ","), 80)
	indexed, err := d.Store.Index(d.Cfg.ProjectKey, source, store.ChunkMarkdown(stdout, store.ChunkMaxBytes))
	if err != nil {
		return ErrorResult("Batch execution error: %v", err)
	}

	lines := []string{
		fmt.Sprintf("Executed %d commands (%d lines, %s). Indexed %d sections. Searched %d queries.",
			len(b.commands), totalLines, KB(len(stdout)), indexed.TotalChunks, len(b.queries)),
		"",
		"## Commands",
		"",
	}
	for _, c := range b.commands {
		lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("- %s: `%s`", c.Label, truncateCommandForEcho(c.Command)))
	}
	lines = append(lines, "", "## Indexed Sections", "")
	for _, s := range indexed.Sections {
		lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("- %s (%s)", s.Title, KB(s.Bytes)))
	}
	lines = append(lines, "")
	qr, err := formatBatchQueryResults(d.Store, d.Cfg.ProjectKey, b.queries, source, batchOutputCap, b.scope)
	if err != nil {
		return ErrorResult("Batch execution error: %v", err)
	}
	lines = append(lines, qr...)
	terms, err := d.Store.DistinctiveTerms(indexed.SourceID, batchTermsMax)
	if err != nil {
		return ErrorResult("Batch execution error: %v", err)
	}
	if len(terms) > 0 {
		lines = append(lines, "\nSearchable terms for follow-up: "+strings.Join(terms, ", "))
	} else {
		lines = append(lines, "")
	}
	// TS S:3810 also has a "Batch timed out after Nms. No output captured."
	// branch; it needs zero outputs, which commands.min(1) makes unreachable.
	return TextResult(strings.Join(lines, "\n"), false)
}

// runBatchCommands runs every command as shell (S:1560-1641). Serial mode
// shares one timeout budget and skips what is left once it runs out; an
// executor error aborts the batch. Parallel mode gives each command the full
// timeout and turns an executor error into that command's section.
func runBatchCommands(ctx context.Context, ex *executor.Executor, b batchArgs) ([]string, error) {
	run := func(c Command, timeout time.Duration) (executor.Result, error) {
		return ex.Run(ctx, executor.Request{Language: executor.Shell, Code: c.Command, Timeout: timeout, Cwd: b.cwd})
	}
	budget := time.Duration(b.timeoutMs * float64(time.Millisecond))

	if b.concurrency <= 1 {
		outputs := make([]string, 0, len(b.commands))
		start := time.Now()
		for i, c := range b.commands {
			var perCmd time.Duration
			if b.hasTimeout {
				remaining := budget - time.Since(start)
				if remaining <= 0 {
					outputs = append(outputs, skippedSection(c.Label))
					continue
				}
				perCmd = remaining
			}
			res, err := run(c, perCmd)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			outputs = append(outputs, formatCommandOutput(c.Label, c.Command, combineExecOutput(res)))
			if res.TimedOut {
				for _, rest := range b.commands[i+1:] {
					outputs = append(outputs, skippedSection(rest.Label))
				}
				break
			}
		}
		return outputs, nil
	}

	var perCmd time.Duration
	timeoutText := "?"
	if b.hasTimeout {
		perCmd = budget
		timeoutText = strconv.FormatFloat(b.timeoutMs, 'f', -1, 64)
	}
	outputs := make([]string, len(b.commands))
	sem := make(chan struct{}, b.concurrency)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i, c := range b.commands {
		wg.Add(1)
		sem <- struct{}{}
		go func() {
			defer func() { <-sem; wg.Done() }()
			res, err := run(c, perCmd)
			if err != nil {
				outputs[i] = fmt.Sprintf("# %s\n\n(executor error: %v)\n", c.Label, err)
				return
			}
			out := formatCommandOutput(c.Label, c.Command, combineExecOutput(res))
			if res.TimedOut {
				out = strings.TrimSuffix(out, "\n") + "\n(timed out after " + timeoutText + "ms)\n"
			}
			outputs[i] = out
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	return outputs, nil
}

func skippedSection(label string) string {
	return "# " + label + "\n\n(skipped — batch timeout exceeded)\n"
}

// formatCommandOutput is S:1529-1543 without the __CM_FS__ byte counter.
func formatCommandOutput(label, command, raw string) string {
	if raw == "" {
		raw = "(no output)"
	}
	return "# " + label + "\n\n$ " + truncateCommandForEcho(command) + "\n\n" + raw + "\n"
}

// combineExecOutput is S:1545-1551.
func combineExecOutput(r executor.Result) string {
	if r.Stderr == "" {
		return r.Stdout
	}
	if r.Stdout == "" {
		return r.Stderr
	}
	sep := "\n"
	if strings.HasSuffix(r.Stdout, "\n") {
		sep = ""
	}
	return r.Stdout + sep + r.Stderr
}

// truncateCommandForEcho is S:1479-1483: collapse whitespace, cap at 500.
func truncateCommandForEcho(command string) string {
	cleaned := strings.Join(strings.Fields(command), " ")
	if jsLen(cleaned) <= commandEchoMax {
		return cleaned
	}
	return jsPrefix(cleaned, commandEchoMax) + "…"
}

// formatBatchQueryResults is S:1371-1418. scope "batch" matches the batch
// source label exactly; "global" searches the whole project.
func formatBatchQueryResults(st *store.Store, project string, queries []string, source string, maxOutput int, scope string) ([]string, error) {
	var sections []string
	outputSize := 0
	opts := store.SearchOpts{Project: project, Limit: batchSearchLimit, Mode: "relevance", SourceExact: true}
	if scope != "global" {
		opts.Source = source
	}
	for _, q := range queries {
		if outputSize > maxOutput {
			sections = append(sections, fmt.Sprintf("## %s\n(output cap reached — use ctx_search(queries: [\"%s\"]) for details)\n", q, q))
			continue
		}
		results, err := st.SearchWithFallback(q, opts)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		sections = append(sections, "## "+q, "")
		if len(results) == 0 {
			sections = append(sections, "No matching sections found.", "")
			continue
		}
		for _, r := range results {
			snippet := store.ExtractSnippet(r.Content, q, batchSnippetMax, r.Highlighted)
			sections = append(sections, "### "+r.Title, snippet, "")
			outputSize += jsLen(snippet) + jsLen(r.Title)
		}
	}
	if scope == "global" {
		sections = append(sections, "\n> **Scope:** Queries searched the entire persistent index (query_scope: \"global\").")
	} else {
		sections = append(sections, "\n> **Tip:** Results are scoped to this batch only. To search across all indexed sources, use `ctx_search(queries: [...])` or call ctx_batch_execute with `query_scope: \"global\"`.")
	}
	return sections, nil
}

// jsLen is JS String.length (UTF-16 code units), used where TS sizes text.
func jsLen(s string) int {
	n := 0
	for _, r := range s {
		n += utf16.RuneLen(r)
	}
	return n
}

// jsPrefix is JS s.slice(0, n) in UTF-16 code units; a split surrogate pair
// is dropped rather than emitted as invalid UTF-8.
func jsPrefix(s string, n int) string {
	units := 0
	for i, r := range s {
		units += utf16.RuneLen(r)
		if units > n {
			return s[:i]
		}
	}
	return s
}
```

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/store"
)

const throttle1 = "\n\n> Throttle: call #1/8 in this window. 2 call(s) before soft cap. Prefer ctx_search(queries: [...]) array form for multi-query workloads — it counts as a single call."

// index stores chunks under label in the harness project.
func (h *harness) index(label string, chunks ...store.Chunk) {
	h.t.Helper()
	if _, err := h.Deps.Store.Index(h.Deps.Cfg.ProjectKey, label, chunks); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// fakeClock returns a clock func and a setter for flood-guard tests.
func fakeClock() (func() time.Time, func(time.Duration)) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 10, 20, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	return func() time.Time { return now }, func(d time.Duration) { now = now.Add(d) }
}

func TestSearchEmptyKnowledgeBase(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["anything"]}`)
	if !isErr || out != emptyKBMessage || !strings.HasPrefix(out, "Knowledge base is empty — no content has been indexed yet.\n\n") {
		t.Fatalf("got isErr=%v %q", isErr, out)
	}
	// Timeline mode skips the guard (S:2599).
	out, isErr = h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["anything"], "sort": "timeline"}`)
	if isErr || out != "## anything\nNo results found."+throttle1 {
		t.Fatalf("timeline on empty KB: isErr=%v %q", isErr, out)
	}
}

func TestSearchMissingQuery(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	h.index("notes", store.Chunk{Title: "Alpha", Content: "gizmo text"})
	for _, args := range []string{`{}`, `{"queries": []}`, `{"query": ""}`} {
		if out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", args); !isErr || out != "Error: provide query or queries." {
			t.Fatalf("%s: got isErr=%v %q", args, isErr, out)
		}
	}
	// Error calls do not consume the flood budget: the next search is call #1.
	if out, _ := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["gizmo"]}`); !strings.HasSuffix(out, throttle1) {
		t.Fatalf("budget consumed by error calls:\n%s", out)
	}
}

func TestSearchOutputFormat(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	h.index("notes", store.Chunk{Title: "Alpha", Content: "gizmo text"})
	out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["gizmo", "qqqqqqqq"]}`)
	if isErr {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %s", out)
	}
	re := regexp.MustCompile(`\A## gizmo\n\n--- \[current-session \| \d{4}-\d\d-\d\d \d\d:\d\d \| notes\] ---\n### Alpha\n\ngizmo text` +
		`\n\n---\n\n## qqqqqqqq\nNo results found.` + regexp.QuoteMeta(throttle1) + `\z`)
	if !re.MatchString(out) {
		t.Fatalf("output:\n%s", out)
	}
	stamp := regexp.MustCompile(`\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d \d\d:\d\d`).FindString(out)
	if at, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02 15:04", stamp); err != nil || time.Since(at) > 5*time.Minute || time.Since(at) < -time.Minute {
		t.Fatalf("timestamp %q is not the UTC index time", stamp)
	}
}

// TS parity (golden t18/t19): the legacy `query` field is stripped by the TS
// zod schema, so it is rejected and does not count against the flood guard.
func TestSearchIgnoresLegacyQueryParam(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	h.index("notes", store.Chunk{Title: "Alpha", Content: "gizmo text"})
	out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"query": "gizmo"}`)
	if !isErr || out != "Error: provide query or queries." {
		t.Fatalf("legacy query should be rejected, got isError=%v:\n%s", isErr, out)
	}
	if out, _ := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["gizmo"]}`); !strings.Contains(out, "call #1/8") {
		t.Fatalf("rejected legacy call must not be counted:\n%s", out)
	}
}

func TestSearchLimitCapping(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	for i := range 5 {
		h.index(fmt.Sprintf("doc-%d", i), store.Chunk{Title: fmt.Sprintf("Doc %d", i), Content: fmt.Sprintf("widget number %d", i)})
	}
	out, _ := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"], "limit": "5"}`)
	if n := strings.Count(out, "--- [current-session"); n != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("limit 5 should be capped to 2 results, got %d:\n%s", n, out)
	}
	out, _ = h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"], "limit": 1}`)
	if n := strings.Count(out, "--- [current-session"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("limit 1: got %d results", n)
	}
	if out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"], "limit": "lots"}`); !isErr || !strings.HasPrefix(out, "Invalid arguments: limit:") {
		t.Fatalf("bad limit: isErr=%v %q", isErr, out)
	}
}

func TestSearchFloodGuard(t *testing.T) {
	now, advance := fakeClock()
	h := newHarness(t, now)
	for i := range 3 {
		h.index(fmt.Sprintf("doc-%d", i), store.Chunk{Title: fmt.Sprintf("Doc %d", i), Content: fmt.Sprintf("widget number %d", i)})
	}
	results := func(out string) int { return strings.Count(out, "--- [current-session") }

	for call := 1; call <= 2; call++ {
		out, _ := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"]}`)
		want := fmt.Sprintf("\n\n> Throttle: call #%d/8 in this window. %d call(s) before soft cap.", call, 3-call)
		if results(out) != 2 || !strings.Contains(out, want) {
			t.Fatalf("call %d:\n%s", call, out)
		}
	}
	// Call 3 warns but still returns 2 per query; call 4 is soft-capped to 1.
	out, _ := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"]}`)
	if results(out) != 2 || !strings.HasSuffix(out, "\n\n⚠ search call #3/8 in this window. Results limited to 2/query. 5 call(s) remaining before block. "+
		`Batch queries: ctx_search(queries: ["q1","q2","q3"]) or use ctx_batch_execute.`) {
		t.Fatalf("call 3:\n%s", out)
	}
	for call := 4; call <= 8; call++ {
		out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"]}`)
		want := fmt.Sprintf("⚠ search call #%d/8 in this window. Results limited to 1/query. %d call(s) remaining before block.", call, 8-call)
		if isErr || results(out) != 1 || !strings.Contains(out, want) {
			t.Fatalf("call %d (isErr=%v):\n%s", call, isErr, out)
		}
	}
	advance(12400 * time.Millisecond)
	out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"]}`)
	if !isErr || out != "BLOCKED: 9 search calls in 12s. You're flooding context. STOP making individual search calls. Use ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries) for your next research step." {
		t.Fatalf("call 9: isErr=%v %q", isErr, out)
	}
	// Still inside the window at exactly +60s from its start.
	advance(60*time.Second - 12400*time.Millisecond)
	if out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"]}`); !isErr || !strings.HasPrefix(out, "BLOCKED: 10 search calls in 60s.") {
		t.Fatalf("at +60s: %q", out)
	}
	advance(time.Second)
	out, isErr = h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["widget"]}`)
	if isErr || results(out) != 2 || !strings.HasSuffix(out, throttle1) {
		t.Fatalf("after window reset (isErr=%v):\n%s", isErr, out)
	}
}

func TestSearchSourceAndContentTypeFilters(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	h.index("alpha-docs", store.Chunk{Title: "A prose", Content: "shared alpha words"}, store.Chunk{Title: "A code", Content: "```go\nshared()\n```", HasCode: true})
	h.index("beta-docs", store.Chunk{Title: "B prose", Content: "shared beta words"})

	out, _ := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["shared"], "source": "lpha"}`)
	if strings.Contains(out, "beta-docs") || strings.Count(out, "| alpha-docs] ---") != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("source LIKE filter:\n%s", out)
	}
	out, _ = h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["shared"], "contentType": "code"}`)
	if strings.Count(out, "--- [") != 1 || !strings.Contains(out, "### A code\n") {
		t.Fatalf("contentType filter:\n%s", out)
	}
	if out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["shared"], "contentType": "binary"}`); !isErr || out != `Invalid arguments: contentType: expected "code" or "prose"` {
		t.Fatalf("bad contentType: %q", out)
	}
}

func TestSearchTimelineSort(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	// Older source is the weaker match, so relevance and timeline disagree.
	h.index("older", store.Chunk{Title: "Old", Content: "timeword " + strings.Repeat("padding filler text ", 40)})
	time.Sleep(1100 * time.Millisecond) // indexed_at has one-second resolution
	h.index("newer", store.Chunk{Title: "timeword", Content: "timeword timeword"})

	order := func(out string) string {
		return strings.Join(regexp.MustCompile(`\| (older|newer)\] ---`).FindAllString(out, -1), ",")
	}
	if got := order(h.call2("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["timeword"]}`)); got != "| newer] ---,| older] ---" {
		t.Fatalf("relevance order: %s", got)
	}
	if got := order(h.call2("ctx_search", `{"queries": ["timeword"], "sort": "timeline"}`)); got != "| older] ---,| newer] ---" {
		t.Fatalf("timeline order: %s", got)
	}
}

// call2 is call without the error flag.
func (h *harness) call2(name, args string) string {
	out, _ := h.call(name, args)
	return out
}

func TestSearchOutputCap(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	for i := range 4 {
		h.index(fmt.Sprintf("big-%d", i), store.Chunk{Title: fmt.Sprintf("Big %d", i), Content: strings.Repeat("capterm lorem ipsum ", 100)})
	}
	qs := make([]string, 20)
	for i := range qs {
		qs[i] = `"capterm"`
	}
	out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": [`+strings.Join(qs, ",")+`]}`)
	if isErr {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %s", out)
	}
	sections := strings.Split(strings.Split(out, "\n\n> Throttle:")[0], "\n\n---\n\n")
	answered, capped := 0, 0
	for _, s := range sections {
		switch {
		case s == "## capterm\n(output cap reached)\n":
			capped++
		case strings.HasPrefix(s, "## capterm\n\n--- [current-session"):
			if capped > 0 {
				t.Fatal("a result section followed a capped one")
			}
			answered++
		default:
			t.Fatalf("unexpected section %q", s)
		}
	}
	// Each answered section is two ~1.5K snippets; the cap is checked before a query runs.
	if answered+capped != 20 || capped == 0 || answered < 12 {
		t.Fatalf("answered=%d capped=%d", answered, capped)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/mcpserver/search.go`:

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"strings"

	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/store"
	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
)

const (
	searchSnippetMax = 1500      // S:2750
	searchOutputCap  = 40 * 1024 // S:2672
)

// searchDescription is S:2564-2585 minus the ctx_stats, CONTEXT_MODE_* env
// and file-staleness sentences, and minus session-memory claims until
// sub-project 3 adds session events (restore them then).
const searchDescription = "Search a unified knowledge base with a multi-strategy ranking pipeline. Two parallel matchers run on every query: a Porter-stemming matcher (\"caching\" finds \"cached\", \"caches\", \"cach\") and a trigram-substring matcher (\"useEff\" finds \"useEffect\"). Their ranked lists are merged via Reciprocal Rank Fusion, so a document that ranks well in both surfaces above one that wins only on a single strategy. Multi-term queries get an additional proximity-rerank pass that boosts passages where the query terms appear close together. Typos are corrected via Levenshtein distance and re-searched. Result snippets are window-extracted around the matched terms, not blindly truncated.\n" +
	"\n" +
	"The knowledge base is unified: queries reach indexed content you stored (ctx_fetch_and_index, ctx_batch_execute output, and large ctx_execute / ctx_execute_file output).\n" +
	"\n" +
	"WHEN:\n" +
	"  - You want to recall something that exists in storage (recently indexed content) instead of re-reading raw sources\n" +
	"  - You have multiple related questions about the same body of knowledge — batch every question into one call (the ranking pipeline runs per-query but the round-trip cost is paid once)\n" +
	"  - You want to scope the query to one labelled source (pass `source` — partial match is fine)\n" +
	"  - You want matches in the order they were indexed, oldest first (pass `sort: \"timeline\"`)\n" +
	"  - You want to filter ranked results by content shape (pass `contentType: \"code\"` to surface implementation snippets or `contentType: \"prose\"` to surface explanations)\n" +
	"\n" +
	"WHEN NOT:\n" +
	"  - The data you want to query has never been stored in the knowledge base — capture first (run a gather-and-index call), then come back here to query\n" +
	"  - You have one ad-hoc question against data that is not in the knowledge base — answer it inline by running code in the sandbox tool; one round-trip instead of capture-then-query\n" +
	"\n" +
	"RETURNS:\n" +
	"  Per-query ranked sections with window-extracted snippets. Use 2-4 specific technical terms per query. Each response carries a throttle counter (call #N/M in the rolling time window); results taper toward the soft cap and calls block after the hard cap.\n" +
	"\n" +
	"EXAMPLE: ctx_search(queries: [\"root cause\", \"proposed fix\", \"test coverage\"], source: \"issue-#683\")\n" +
	"EXAMPLE: ctx_search(queries: [\"useEffect cleanup pattern\"], source: \"react-docs\", contentType: \"code\", limit: 5)"

// searchSchema mirrors buildCtxSearchInputSchema (ctx-search-schema.ts:62-105)
// without the shared-DB `project` field.
const searchSchema = `{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "queries": {"type": ["array", "string"], "items": {"type": "string"}, "description": "Array of search queries. Batch ALL questions in one call."},
    "limit": {"type": ["number", "string"], "default": 3, "description": "Results per query (default: 3)"},
    "source": {"type": "string", "description": "Filter to a specific indexed source (partial match)."},
    "contentType": {"type": "string", "enum": ["code", "prose"], "description": "Filter results by content type: 'code' or 'prose'."},
    "sort": {"type": "string", "enum": ["relevance", "timeline"], "default": "relevance", "description": "Sort mode. 'relevance' (default): BM25 ranked. 'timeline': the same matches ordered oldest-indexed first."}
  }
}`

const emptyKBMessage = "Knowledge base is empty — no content has been indexed yet.\n\n" +
	"ctx_search is a follow-up tool that queries previously indexed content. " +
	"To gather and index content first, use:\n" +
	"  • ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries) — run commands, auto-index output, and search in one call\n" +
	"  • ctx_fetch_and_index(url) — fetch a URL, index it, then search with ctx_search\n" +
	"  • ctx_index(content, source) — manually index text content\n\n" +
	"After indexing, ctx_search becomes available for follow-up queries."

func registerSearch(s *mcp.Server, d *Deps) {
	guard := &floodGuard{} // one server per process, so this is process-wide
	s.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
		Name:        "ctx_search",
		Title:       "Search Indexed Content",
		Description: searchDescription,
		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(searchSchema),
		Annotations: &mcp.ToolAnnotations{ReadOnlyHint: true, IdempotentHint: true, DestructiveHint: new(bool), OpenWorldHint: new(bool)},
	}, func(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
		return searchTool(d, guard, req.Params.Arguments), nil
	})
}

type searchArgs struct {
	queries     []string
	limit       float64
	source      string
	contentType store.ContentType
	sort        string
}

func parseSearchArgs(raw json.RawMessage) (searchArgs, error) {
	sa := searchArgs{limit: 3, sort: "relevance"}
	a, err := ParseArgs(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return sa, err
	}
	if sa.queries, _, err = a.Strings("queries"); err != nil {
		return sa, err
	}
	// No legacy `query`: TS reads it (S:2621) but its zod schema has no such
	// field, so the SDK strips it and TS answers "provide query or queries".
	if n, ok, err := a.Number("limit"); err != nil {
		return sa, err
	} else if ok {
		sa.limit = n
	}
	if sa.source, _, err = a.String("source"); err != nil {
		return sa, err
	}
	if ct, ok, err := a.String("contentType"); err != nil {
		return sa, err
	} else if ok {
		if ct != string(store.Code) && ct != string(store.Prose) {
			return sa, errors.New(`contentType: expected "code" or "prose"`)
		}
		sa.contentType = store.ContentType(ct)
	}
	if so, ok, err := a.String("sort"); err != nil {
		return sa, err
	} else if ok {
		if so != "relevance" && so != "timeline" {
			return sa, errors.New(`sort: expected "relevance" or "timeline"`)
		}
		sa.sort = so
	}
	return sa, nil
}

// searchTool is the ctx_search handler (S:2592-2805) over the content store only.
func searchTool(d *Deps, guard *floodGuard, raw json.RawMessage) *mcp.CallToolResult {
	sa, err := parseSearchArgs(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return ArgError(err)
	}
	project := d.Cfg.ProjectKey
	if sa.sort != "timeline" {
		n, err := d.Store.ChunkCount(project)
		if err != nil {
			return ErrorResult("Search error: %v", err)
		}
		if n == 0 {
			return TextResult(emptyKBMessage, true)
		}
	}
	if len(sa.queries) == 0 {
		return TextResult("Error: provide query or queries.", true)
	}

	now := d.Now()
	flood := guard.record(now)
	if flood.blocked {
		return ErrorResult("BLOCKED: %d search calls in %ds. "+
			"You're flooding context. STOP making individual search calls. "+
			"Use ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries) for your next research step.",
			flood.count, int(math.Round(now.Sub(flood.windowStart).Seconds())))
	}
	effectiveLimit := 1
	if !flood.softCapped {
		effectiveLimit = int(math.Min(sa.limit, 2)) // JS slice(0, 1.5) keeps 1
	}

	opts := store.SearchOpts{Project: project, Source: sa.source, ContentType: sa.contentType, Limit: effectiveLimit, Mode: sa.sort}
	totalSize := 0
	var sections []string
	for _, q := range sa.queries {
		if totalSize > searchOutputCap {
			sections = append(sections, "## "+q+"\n(output cap reached)\n")
			continue
		}
		var results []store.SearchResult
		if effectiveLimit > 0 { // TS slice(0, <=0) is empty; the store would default to 3
			if results, err = d.Store.SearchWithFallback(q, opts); err != nil {
				return ErrorResult("Search error: %v", err)
			}
		}
		if len(results) == 0 {
			sections = append(sections, "## "+q+"\nNo results found.")
			continue
		}
		parts := make([]string, len(results))
		for i, r := range results {
			ts := ""
			if !r.IndexedAt.IsZero() {
				ts = " | " + r.IndexedAt.UTC().Format("2006-01-02 15:04")
			}
			parts[i] = fmt.Sprintf("--- [current-session%s | %s] ---\n### %s\n\n%s",
				ts, r.Source, r.Title, store.ExtractSnippet(r.Content, q, searchSnippetMax, r.Highlighted))
		}
		formatted := strings.Join(parts, "\n\n")
		sections = append(sections, "## "+q+"\n\n"+formatted)
		totalSize += jsLen(formatted)
	}
	output := strings.Join(sections, "\n\n---\n\n")

	if flood.count >= searchSoftCapAt {
		output += fmt.Sprintf("\n\n⚠ search call #%d/%d in this window. "+
			"Results limited to %d/query. %d call(s) remaining before block. "+
			`Batch queries: ctx_search(queries: ["q1","q2","q3"]) or use ctx_batch_execute.`,
			flood.count, searchBlockAfter, effectiveLimit, max(0, searchBlockAfter-flood.count))
	} else {
		output += fmt.Sprintf("\n\n> Throttle: call #%d/%d in this window. "+
			"%d call(s) before soft cap. "+
			"Prefer ctx_search(queries: [...]) array form for multi-query workloads — it counts as a single call.",
			flood.count, searchBlockAfter, max(0, searchSoftCapAt-flood.count))
	}
	// TS S:2785 falls back to "No results found. Indexed sources: …" when the
	// output is blank; the throttle line above makes that unreachable.
	return TextResult(output, false)
}
```

`internal/mcpserver/review_search_test.go`:

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/store"
)

// Review focus: models paste raw text into ctx_search — FTS5 operators,
// unbalanced quotes, column filters, only stopwords. None may surface an SQL
// error; each query gets a normal section.
func TestSearchSurvivesFTSSyntax(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	h.index("notes", store.Chunk{Title: "Alpha", Content: "the gizmo handles NEAR misses and col:value pairs"})
	queries := []string{`"gizmo`, `gizmo*`, `NEAR(gizmo misses)`, `title:gizmo`, `the and of`, `^gizmo`, `gizmo AND OR NOT`, `(((`, `'; DROP TABLE sources; --`}
	for i, q := range queries {
		if i > 0 && i%3 == 0 {
			h = newHarness(t, nil) // stay under the flood-guard soft cap per harness
			h.index("notes", store.Chunk{Title: "Alpha", Content: "the gizmo handles NEAR misses and col:value pairs"})
		}
		out, isErr := h.call("ctx_search", `{"queries": [`+jsonString(q)+`]}`)
		if isErr || strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(out), "sql") || strings.Contains(out, "fts5") {
			t.Errorf("query %q: isError=%v\n%s", q, isErr, out)
		}
		if !strings.HasPrefix(out, "## "+q) {
			t.Errorf("query %q: missing section header\n%s", q, out)
		}
	}
}

func jsonString(s string) string {
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteByte('"')
	for _, r := range s {
		switch r {
		case '"':
			b.WriteString(`\"`)
		case '\\':
			b.WriteString(`\\`)
		default:
			b.WriteRune(r)
		}
	}
	b.WriteByte('"')
	return b.String()
}
```

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestFetchCacheKey(t *testing.T) {
	if got := fetchCacheKey("", false, "https://x/a"); got != "https://x/a" {
		t.Fatalf("no source: %q", got)
	}
	if got := fetchCacheKey("docs", true, "https://x/a"); got != "docs::https://x/a" {
		t.Fatalf("source: %q", got)
	}
	if got := fetchCacheKey("", true, "https://x/a"); got != "::https://x/a" {
		t.Fatalf("empty source is still a source in TS: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestFetchAgeAndTTL(t *testing.T) {
	ages := map[int64]string{0: "just now", 59_999: "just now", 60_000: "1m ago", 3_599_999: "59m ago", 3_600_000: "1h ago", 90 * 60_000: "1h ago", -5000: "just now"}
	for ms, want := range ages {
		if got := fetchAge(ms); got != want {
			t.Errorf("fetchAge(%d) = %q, want %q", ms, got, want)
		}
	}
	ttls := map[int64]string{0: "0ms", 86_400_000: "1d", 172_800_000: "2d", 7_200_000: "2h", 300_000: "5m", 1500: "1500ms", 90_000_000: "25h"}
	for ms, want := range ttls {
		if got := fetchTTL(ms); got != want {
			t.Errorf("fetchTTL(%d) = %q, want %q", ms, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestFetchPreview(t *testing.T) {
	short := strings.Repeat("a", fetchPreviewLimit)
	if fetchPreview(short) != short {
		t.Fatal("body at the limit must be kept whole")
	}
	long := strings.Repeat("é", fetchPreviewLimit+10)
	want := strings.Repeat("é", fetchPreviewLimit) + "\n\n…[truncated — use ctx_search() for full content]"
	if got := fetchPreview(long); got != want {
		t.Fatalf("truncated preview wrong: len %d", len(got))
	}
}

func TestFetchPoolSize(t *testing.T) {
	defer func(f func() int) { fetchNumCPU = f }(fetchNumCPU)
	fetchNumCPU = func() int { return 2 }
	cases := []struct {
		req, jobs int
		byCPU     bool
		eff       int
		capped    bool
	}{
		{1, 3, false, 1, false},
		{4, 3, true, 2, true},  // CPU cap
		{8, 3, false, 3, true}, // job-count clamp also reports capped (runPool.ts)
		{2, 5, true, 2, false},
		{3, 1, false, 1, true},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		eff, capped := fetchPoolSize(c.req, c.jobs, c.byCPU)
		if eff != c.eff || capped != c.capped {
			t.Errorf("fetchPoolSize(%d,%d,%v) = %d,%v want %d,%v", c.req, c.jobs, c.byCPU, eff, capped, c.eff, c.capped)
		}
	}
}

func TestFetchSingleText(t *testing.T) {
	cached, isErr := fetchSingleText(fetchOutcome{kind: "cached", label: "docs::u", chunks: 4, age: "2h ago", ttl: "1d"})
	wantCached := "Cached: **docs::u** — 4 sections, indexed 2h ago (fresh, TTL: 1d).\nTo refresh: call ctx_fetch_and_index again with `force: true`.\n\nYou MUST call ctx_search() to answer questions about this content — this cached response contains no content.\nUse: ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"docs::u\")"
	if cached != wantCached || isErr {
		t.Fatalf("cached text:\n%s", cached)
	}
	fetched, isErr := fetchSingleText(fetchOutcome{kind: "fetched", label: "u", chunks: 2, bytes: 2048, preview: "# Hi"})
	wantFetched := "Fetched and indexed **2 sections** (2.0KB) from: u\nFull content indexed in sandbox — use ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"u\") for specific lookups.\n\n---\n\n# Hi"
	if fetched != wantFetched || isErr {
		t.Fatalf("fetched text:\n%s", fetched)
	}
	errs := map[fetchOutcome]string{
		{kind: "error", url: "u", reason: "empty", err: "empty content"}: "Fetched u but got empty content",
		{kind: "error", url: "u", reason: "exit", err: "HTTP 404"}:       "Failed to fetch u: HTTP 404",
		{kind: "error", url: "u", reason: "throw", err: "disk full"}:     "Fetch error: disk full",
	}
	for o, want := range errs {
		if got, isErr := fetchSingleText(o); got != want || !isErr {
			t.Errorf("error text %q (isError %v), want %q", got, isErr, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestFetchBatchText(t *testing.T) {
	defer func(f func() int) { fetchNumCPU = f }(fetchNumCPU)
	fetchNumCPU = func() int { return 2 }
	long := strings.Repeat("abc ", 125) // 500 chars; char 384 is a space, so the cut is trimmed
	outs := []fetchOutcome{
		{kind: "fetched", label: "a", chunks: 1, bytes: 1024, preview: long},
		{kind: "cached", label: "b", chunks: 3, age: "just now", ttl: "1d"},
		{kind: "error", url: "http://c", err: "HTTP 404"},
	}
	got, isErr := fetchBatchText(outs, 2, true)
	want := "fetched 3 c=2 cap=2/2cpu. ok=1 cache=1 err=1. 1 section 1.0KB.\n\n" +
		"- [new]   a — 1 sections (1.0KB)\n" +
		"- [cache] b — 3 sections (just now, TTL: 1d)\n" +
		"- [err]   http://c: HTTP 404\n\n" +
		"ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"<label>\") for full content.\n\n---\n\n" +
		"### a\n\n" + strings.TrimSpace(long[:384]) + "…"
	if got != want || isErr {
		t.Fatalf("batch text:\n%s\nwant:\n%s", got, want)
	}
	allErr, isErr := fetchBatchText(outs[2:], 1, false)
	if !isErr || !strings.HasPrefix(allErr, "fetched 1 c=1. ok=0 cache=0 err=1. 0 sections 0.0KB.\n") || strings.Contains(allErr, "---") {
		t.Fatalf("all-error batch (isError %v):\n%s", isErr, allErr)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/mcpserver/fetchindex_format.go`:

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"fmt"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
	"unicode"
)

const (
	fetchTTLDefaultMs   = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // FETCH_TTL_MS, S:3118
	fetchPreviewLimit   = 3072                // FETCH_PREVIEW_LIMIT, S:3119
	fetchBatchPreview   = 384                 // FETCH_BATCH_PREVIEW_LIMIT, S:3622
	fetchTruncateSuffix = "\n\n…[truncated — use ctx_search() for full content]"
)

// fetchNumCPU is os.cpus().length; a var so tests can pin the CPU cap.
var fetchNumCPU = runtime.NumCPU

// fetchOutcome is one URL's final state (TS Finalized, S:3536-3540).
type fetchOutcome struct {
	kind    string // "cached" | "fetched" | "error"
	url     string
	label   string
	chunks  int
	age     string // cached
	ttl     string // cached
	bytes   int    // fetched
	preview string // fetched
	err     string // error
	reason  string // error: "exit" | "empty" | "throw"
}

// fetchCacheKey is composeFetchCacheKey, fetch-cache.ts:13-15.
func fetchCacheKey(source string, hasSource bool, url string) string {
	if !hasSource {
		return url
	}
	return source + "::" + url
}

// fetchAge formats a cache age in ms, S:3307-3310.
func fetchAge(ageMs int64) string {
	if h := ageMs / (60 * 60 * 1000); h > 0 {
		return fmt.Sprintf("%dh ago", h)
	}
	if m := ageMs / (60 * 1000); m > 0 {
		return fmt.Sprintf("%dm ago", m)
	}
	return "just now"
}

// fetchTTL is formatFetchTtl, S:3121-3131.
func fetchTTL(ttlMs int64) string {
	const minute, hour, day = 60 * 1000, 60 * 60 * 1000, 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
	switch {
	case ttlMs == 0:
		return "0ms"
	case ttlMs%day == 0:
		return fmt.Sprintf("%dd", ttlMs/day)
	case ttlMs%hour == 0:
		return fmt.Sprintf("%dh", ttlMs/hour)
	case ttlMs%minute == 0:
		return fmt.Sprintf("%dm", ttlMs/minute)
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("%dms", ttlMs)
}

// fetchPreview is the preview cut in indexFetched, S:3400-3402.
// shortcut: counts runes where TS counts UTF-16 units; differs only for astral characters.
func fetchPreview(body string) string {
	r := []rune(body)
	if len(r) <= fetchPreviewLimit {
		return body
	}
	return string(r[:fetchPreviewLimit]) + fetchTruncateSuffix
}

// fetchPoolSize is runPool's sizing, runPool.ts:42-55: capped is also true
// when the job count, not the CPU count, lowered the value.
func fetchPoolSize(requested, jobs int, capByCPU bool) (eff int, capped bool) {
	requested = max(1, requested)
	cpuCap := requested
	if capByCPU {
		cpuCap = max(1, fetchNumCPU())
	}
	eff = min(requested, cpuCap, jobs)
	return eff, eff < requested
}

// fetchSingleText is the legacy single-URL response, S:3573-3617.
func fetchSingleText(o fetchOutcome) (string, bool) {
	switch o.kind {
	case "cached":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Cached: **%s** — %d sections, indexed %s (fresh, TTL: %s).\nTo refresh: call ctx_fetch_and_index again with `force: true`.\n\nYou MUST call ctx_search() to answer questions about this content — this cached response contains no content.\nUse: ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"%s\")",
			o.label, o.chunks, o.age, o.ttl, o.label), false
	case "fetched":
		return strings.Join([]string{
			fmt.Sprintf("Fetched and indexed **%d sections** (%s) from: %s", o.chunks, KB(o.bytes), o.label),
			fmt.Sprintf("Full content indexed in sandbox — use ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"%s\") for specific lookups.", o.label),
			"",
			"---",
			"",
			o.preview,
		}, "\n"), false
	}
	switch o.reason {
	case "empty":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Fetched %s but got empty content", o.url), true
	case "exit":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Failed to fetch %s: %s", o.url, o.err), true
	}
	return "Fetch error: " + o.err, true
}

// fetchBatchText is the aggregated batch response, S:3619-3671; isError
// only when every URL failed.
func fetchBatchText(outs []fetchOutcome, eff int, capped bool) (string, bool) {
	var lines, snippets []string
	var sections, bytes, cached, fetched, errs int
	for _, o := range outs {
		switch o.kind {
		case "cached":
			cached++
			lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("- [cache] %s — %d sections (%s, TTL: %s)", o.label, o.chunks, o.age, o.ttl))
		case "fetched":
			fetched++
			sections += o.chunks
			bytes += o.bytes
			lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("- [new]   %s — %d sections (%s)", o.label, o.chunks, KB(o.bytes)))
			snippet := o.preview
			if r := []rune(snippet); len(r) > fetchBatchPreview {
				snippet = strings.TrimRightFunc(string(r[:fetchBatchPreview]), unicode.IsSpace) + "…"
			}
			snippets = append(snippets, fmt.Sprintf("### %s\n\n%s", o.label, snippet))
		default:
			errs++
			lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("- [err]   %s: %s", o.url, o.err))
		}
	}
	capNote := ""
	if capped {
		capNote = fmt.Sprintf(" cap=%d/%dcpu", eff, fetchNumCPU())
	}
	noun := "sections"
	if sections == 1 {
		noun = "section"
	}
	header := fmt.Sprintf("fetched %d c=%d%s. ok=%d cache=%d err=%d. %d %s %s.",
		len(outs), eff, capNote, fetched, cached, errs, sections, noun, KB(bytes))
	text := append([]string{header, ""}, lines...)
	text = append(text, "", `ctx_search(queries: [...], source: "<label>") for full content.`)
	if len(snippets) > 0 {
		text = append(append(text, "", "---", ""), snippets...)
	}
	return strings.Join(text, "\n"), errs == len(outs)
}
```

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"regexp"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/fetch"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/store"
)

// fetchSite serves fixed pages on loopback and counts hits per path.
type fetchSite struct {
	*httptest.Server
	hits map[string]*atomic.Int32
}

func newFetchSite(t *testing.T) *fetchSite {
	t.Helper()
	pages := map[string][2]string{ // path -> {content type, body}
		"/page":      {"text/html; charset=utf-8", "<html><head><title>T</title><script>x()</script></head><body><h1>Alpha Guide</h1><p>The zebrafish protocol.</p></body></html>"},
		"/data.json": {"application/json", `{"alpha":{"beta":"zebrafish"},"gamma":[1,2]}`},
		"/bad.json":  {"application/json", `{not json zebrafish`},
		"/plain":     {"text/plain", "line one zebrafish\nline two"},
		"/empty":     {"text/plain", "  \n "},
	}
	s := &fetchSite{hits: map[string]*atomic.Int32{}}
	for p := range pages {
		s.hits[p] = &atomic.Int32{}
	}
	s.Server = httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		pg, ok := pages[r.URL.Path]
		if !ok {
			http.NotFound(w, r)
			return
		}
		s.hits[r.URL.Path].Add(1)
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", pg[0])
		fmt.Fprint(w, pg[1])
	}))
	t.Cleanup(s.Close)
	return s
}

// skewedHarness returns a harness whose clock is time.Now()+*skew.
func skewedHarness(t *testing.T) (*harness, *time.Duration) {
	skew := new(time.Duration)
	return newHarness(t, func() time.Time { return time.Now().Add(*skew) }), skew
}

func searchSource(t *testing.T, h *harness, q, label string) []store.SearchResult {
	t.Helper()
	res, err := h.Deps.Store.SearchWithFallback(q, store.SearchOpts{Project: h.Deps.Cfg.ProjectKey, Source: label, SourceExact: true, Limit: 3, Mode: "relevance"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return res
}

func TestFetchAndIndexSingleHTML(t *testing.T) {
	site := newFetchSite(t)
	h, _ := skewedHarness(t)
	u := site.URL + "/page"
	text, isErr := h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", fmt.Sprintf(`{"url": %q}`, u))
	want := "Fetched and indexed **1 sections** (0.0KB) from: " + u + "\n" +
		"Full content indexed in sandbox — use ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"" + u + "\") for specific lookups.\n\n---\n\n" +
		"# Alpha Guide\n\nThe zebrafish protocol."
	if isErr || text != want {
		t.Fatalf("isError=%v text:\n%s", isErr, text)
	}
	res := searchSource(t, h, "zebrafish", u)
	if len(res) == 0 || res[0].Title != "Alpha Guide" || res[0].Source != u {
		t.Fatalf("not searchable: %+v", res)
	}
}

func TestFetchAndIndexCacheTTLForce(t *testing.T) {
	site := newFetchSite(t)
	h, skew := skewedHarness(t)
	u := site.URL + "/page"
	call := func(extra string) string {
		t.Helper()
		text, isErr := h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", fmt.Sprintf(`{"url": %q%s}`, u, extra))
		if isErr {
			t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %s", text)
		}
		return text
	}
	call("")

	got := call("")
	want := "Cached: **" + u + "** — 1 sections, indexed just now (fresh, TTL: 1d).\nTo refresh: call ctx_fetch_and_index again with `force: true`.\n\nYou MUST call ctx_search() to answer questions about this content — this cached response contains no content.\nUse: ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"" + u + "\")"
	if got != want {
		t.Fatalf("cached text:\n%s", got)
	}
	*skew = 90 * time.Minute
	if got := call(""); !strings.HasPrefix(got, "Cached: **"+u+"** — 1 sections, indexed 1h ago (fresh, TTL: 1d).") {
		t.Fatalf("aged cache text:\n%s", got)
	}
	if got := call(`, "ttl": "7200000"`); !strings.Contains(got, "indexed 1h ago (fresh, TTL: 2h)") {
		t.Fatalf("ttl override text:\n%s", got)
	}
	if n := site.hits["/page"].Load(); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("cache hits must not fetch; hits=%d", n)
	}

	for i, extra := range []string{`, "force": true`, `, "ttl": 0`, `, "ttl": 3600000`, `, "force": "true"`} {
		if got := call(extra); !strings.HasPrefix(got, "Fetched and indexed **1 sections**") {
			t.Fatalf("%s must refetch:\n%s", extra, got)
		}
		if n := site.hits["/page"].Load(); n != int32(i+2) {
			t.Fatalf("%s: hits=%d", extra, n)
		}
		*skew = 90 * time.Minute // a refetch restamps the source; keep it 90m old for the stale-ttl case
	}
}

func TestFetchAndIndexArgs(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := skewedHarness(t)
	if text, isErr := h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", `{}`); !isErr || text != "ctx_fetch_and_index requires either `url` (single) or `requests: [{url, source?}, ...]` (batch)." {
		t.Fatalf("missing url: %v %q", isErr, text)
	}
	for _, args := range []string{`{"requests": []}`, `{"url": "http://x", "concurrency": 9}`, `{"url": "http://x", "ttl": -1}`, `{"url": "http://x", "ttl": 1.5}`, `{"url": "http://x", "force": "yes"}`, `{"requests": [{"source": "s"}]}`} {
		if text, isErr := h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", args); !isErr || !strings.HasPrefix(text, "Invalid arguments: ") {

		}

func TestFetchAndIndexBatch(t *testing.T) {
	site := newFetchSite(t)
	h, _ := skewedHarness(t)
	page, plain, missing := site.URL+"/page", site.URL+"/plain", site.URL+"/missing"
	h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", fmt.Sprintf(`{"url": %q}`, plain)) // warm the cache for one entry
	args := fmt.Sprintf(`{"requests": [{"url": %q, "source": "docs"}, {"url": %q}, {"url": %q}], "concurrency": 2}`, page, plain, missing)
	text, isErr := h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", args)
	eff := min(2, runtime.NumCPU())
	capNote := ""
	if eff < 2 {
		capNote = fmt.Sprintf(" cap=%d/%dcpu", eff, runtime.NumCPU())
	}
	want := fmt.Sprintf("fetched 3 c=%d%s. ok=1 cache=1 err=1. 1 section 0.0KB.\n\n", eff, capNote) +
		"- [new]   docs::" + page + " — 1 sections (0.0KB)\n" +
		"- [cache] " + plain + " — 1 sections (just now, TTL: 1d)\n" +
		"- [err]   " + missing + ": HTTP 404\n\n" +
		"ctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"<label>\") for full content.\n\n---\n\n" +
		"### docs::" + page + "\n\n# Alpha Guide\n\nThe zebrafish protocol."
	if isErr || text != want {
		t.Fatalf("isError=%v text:\n%s", isErr, text)
	}

	// One entry in requests still gets the batch shape.
	text, isErr = h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", fmt.Sprintf(`{"requests": [{"url": %q}]}`, missing))
	if !isErr || text != "fetched 1 c=1. ok=0 cache=0 err=1. 0 sections 0.0KB.\n\n- [err]   "+missing+": HTTP 404\n\nctx_search(queries: [...], source: \"<label>\") for full content." {
		t.Fatalf("all-failing batch: isError=%v text:\n%s", isErr, text)
	}
}

func TestFetchAndIndexConcurrencyCap(t *testing.T) {
	site := newFetchSite(t)
	h, _ := skewedHarness(t)
	reqs := fmt.Sprintf(`[{"url": %q}, {"url": %q}, {"url": %q}]`, site.URL+"/page", site.URL+"/plain", site.URL+"/data.json")
	header := regexp.MustCompile(`^fetched 3 c=(\d+)( cap=\d+/\d+cpu)?\. `)

	defer func(f func() int) { fetchNumCPU = f }(fetchNumCPU)
	fetchNumCPU = func() int { return 2 } // the schema max (8) is below this machine's CPU count, so pin it
	text, _ := h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", fmt.Sprintf(`{"requests": %s, "concurrency": 4, "force": true}`, reqs))
	if m := header.FindStringSubmatch(text); m == nil || m[1] != "2" || m[2] != " cap=2/2cpu" {
		t.Fatalf("CPU cap header:\n%s", text)
	}

	fetchNumCPU = runtime.NumCPU
	text, _ = h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", fmt.Sprintf(`{"requests": %s, "concurrency": "8", "force": true}`, reqs))
	eff := min(8, runtime.NumCPU(), 3) // runPool also reports the job-count clamp as capped
	if m := header.FindStringSubmatch(text); m == nil || m[1] != fmt.Sprint(eff) || m[2] != fmt.Sprintf(" cap=%d/%dcpu", eff, runtime.NumCPU()) {
		t.Fatalf("job clamp header:\n%s", text)
	}
}

func TestFetchAndIndexStrictSSRF(t *testing.T) {
	site := newFetchSite(t)
	h, _ := skewedHarness(t)
	h.Deps.Fetch = fetch.New(true)
	u := site.URL + "/page"
	text, isErr := h.call("ctx_fetch_and_index", fmt.Sprintf(`{"url": %q}`, u))
	if !isErr || text != "Failed to fetch "+u+`: URL "127.0.0.1" resolves to private IP 127.0.0.1 — blocked under CMGO_FETCH_STRICT=1` {
		t.Fatalf("isError=%v text:\n%s", isErr, text)
	}
	if n := site.hits["/page"].Load(); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("strict mode must not reach the server; hits=%d", n)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`internal/mcpserver/fetchindex.go`:

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"sync"

	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/fetch"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/store"
	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
)

// S:3418-3445, minus the "inline index tool" WHEN NOT bullet (cmgo has no ctx_index).
const fetchDescription = "Fetches URL content, converts HTML to markdown (JSON is chunked by key paths, plain text indexed directly), persists it in a searchable knowledge base, and returns a small preview window per source. The raw page bytes never enter your conversation — they live in storage and you retrieve any section on-demand via ctx_search.\n\n" +
	"Caching: every fetch is cached on disk and reused for repeat calls within the TTL window. The default TTL is 24 hours; override per-call with the `ttl` parameter (milliseconds, `ttl: 0` bypasses cache like `force: true`). Stored content older than 14 days is cleaned up on startup.\n\n" +
	"WHEN:\n" +
	"  - You need web content (docs, changelogs, API references, spec pages) and the raw page bytes should NOT enter your conversation\n" +
	"  - Multi-URL research (library evaluation, migration scans, doc comparisons): pass the `requests` array and a `concurrency` value 2-8 for parallel I/O\n" +
	"  - You want repeat lookups against the same URL to be cheap (TTL cache hits return only a hint, no re-fetch)\n" +
	"  - You want a long-lived cache window (override `ttl` upward for stable specs) or a guaranteed-fresh fetch (`ttl: 0` or `force: true`)\n\n" +
	"WHEN NOT:\n" +
	"  - The page is SPA-rendered (JavaScript-required to materialize content) — this is a plain HTTP fetch, no headless browser\n\n" +
	"RETURNS:\n" +
	"  Per-source preview windows extracted around indexable headings plus indexing metadata (chunk counts, source labels, cache state). Raw content is NOT echoed back — retrieve any section on-demand via ctx_search(source: \"<label>\"). Concurrency parallelizes the fetch phase up to your chosen value (capped by the host's logical CPU count); the FTS5 write phase always runs serially because SQLite is a single-writer store. Net latency = max(fetch latency across the pool) + sum(per-source index write time). Cache hits skip both phases and return a small freshness hint instead of re-fetching. Use 4-8 for stable I/O-bound batches; lower the value when the target host enforces a per-IP rate limit you cannot raise.\n\n" +
	"EXAMPLE: ctx_fetch_and_index(\n" +
	"  requests: [{url: \"https://react.dev/...\", source: \"react\"}, {url: \"https://vuejs.org/...\", source: \"vue\"}],\n" +
	"  concurrency: 5\n" +
	")"

// S:3446-3498 (zod schema), with coerced fields widened to accept strings.
const fetchSchema = `{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "url": {"type": "string", "description": "Single URL to fetch and index (legacy single-shape)"},
    "source": {"type": "string", "description": "Label for the indexed content when using single ` + "`url`" + ` (e.g., 'React useEffect docs', 'Supabase Auth API'). For batch, put source in each requests entry."},
    "requests": {
      "type": ["array", "string"],
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "url": {"type": "string", "description": "URL to fetch"},
          "source": {"type": "string", "description": "Label for this URL's indexed content"}
        },
        "required": ["url"]
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "description": "Batch shape: array of {url, source?} entries. Use with concurrency>1 for parallel fetch. Each request indexed under its own source label. Output preserves input order."
    },
    "concurrency": {"type": ["integer", "string"], "minimum": 1, "maximum": 8, "default": 1, "description": "Max URLs to fetch in parallel (1-8, default: 1). Use 4-8 for I/O-bound multi-URL batches (library docs, changelogs, pricing pages). Capped by os.cpus().length on small machines (response notes when capped). Indexing is always serial regardless — only fetches race."},
    "force": {"type": ["boolean", "string"], "description": "Skip cache and re-fetch even if content was recently indexed"},
    "ttl": {"type": ["integer", "string"], "minimum": 0, "description": "Override the cache freshness window for this call, in milliseconds. ` + "`ttl: 0`" + ` bypasses the cache like ` + "`force: true`" + `; omit to use the default 24h TTL."}
  }
}`

type fetchRequest struct {
	url, source string
	hasSource   bool
}

// fetchJob is the parallel-safe half of one URL (fetchOneUrl, S:3288-3370):
// either a final outcome (cached, error) or a body still to index.
type fetchJob struct {
	out  fetchOutcome
	body fetch.Result
}

func registerFetchAndIndex(s *mcp.Server, d *Deps) {
	no := false
	yes := true
	s.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
		Name:        "ctx_fetch_and_index",
		Title:       "Fetch & Index URL(s)",
		Description: fetchDescription,
		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(fetchSchema),
		Annotations: &mcp.ToolAnnotations{DestructiveHint: &no, OpenWorldHint: &yes},
	}, func(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
		return handleFetchAndIndex(ctx, d, req.Params.Arguments), nil
	})
}

// handleFetchAndIndex is the tool handler, S:3499-3671.
func handleFetchAndIndex(ctx context.Context, d *Deps, raw json.RawMessage) *mcp.CallToolResult {
	a, err := ParseArgs(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return ArgError(err)
	}
	batch, hasRequests, err := fetchRequests(a)
	if err != nil {
		return ArgError(err)
	}
	concurrency, err := fetchInt(a, "concurrency", 1, 1, 8)
	if err != nil {
		return ArgError(err)
	}
	ttl, err := fetchInt(a, "ttl", -1, 0, math.MaxInt64)
	if err != nil {
		return ArgError(err)
	}
	force, _, err := a.Bool("force")
	if err != nil {
		return ArgError(err)
	}
	if len(batch) == 0 {
		return TextResult("ctx_fetch_and_index requires either `url` (single) or `requests: [{url, source?}, ...]` (batch).", true)
	}

	single := !hasRequests && len(batch) == 1
	eff, capped := fetchPoolSize(int(concurrency), len(batch), !single && concurrency > 1)

	// Parallel fetch with eff workers; results land by index.
	jobs := make([]fetchJob, len(batch))
	sem := make(chan struct{}, eff)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i, r := range batch {
		wg.Add(1)
		sem <- struct{}{}
		go func() {
			defer func() { <-sem; wg.Done() }()
			jobs[i] = fetchOne(ctx, d, r, force, ttl)
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()

	// Serial index drain in request order, S:3542-3570.
	outs := make([]fetchOutcome, len(jobs))
	for i, j := range jobs {
		if j.out.kind != "" {
			outs[i] = j.out
			continue
		}
		outs[i] = fetchIndex(d, batch[i], j.body)
	}

	var text string
	var isErr bool
	if single {
		text, isErr = fetchSingleText(outs[0])
	} else {
		text, isErr = fetchBatchText(outs, eff, capped)
	}
	return TextResult(text, isErr)
}

// fetchRequests normalizes {url, source} and requests[] (requests wins), S:3502-3506.
func fetchRequests(a Args) ([]fetchRequest, bool, error) {
	arr, hasRequests, err := a.Array("requests")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	}
	if hasRequests {
		if len(arr) == 0 {
			return nil, true, errors.New("requests: expected at least 1 entry")
		}
		out := make([]fetchRequest, 0, len(arr))
		for i, e := range arr {
			m, ok := e.(map[string]any)
			if !ok {
				return nil, true, fmt.Errorf("requests[%d]: expected object", i)
			}
			ra := Args(m)
			u, hasURL, err := ra.String("url")
			if err != nil || !hasURL {
				return nil, true, fmt.Errorf("requests[%d].url: expected string", i)
			}
			src, hasSrc, err := ra.String("source")
			if err != nil {
				return nil, true, fmt.Errorf("requests[%d].%v", i, err)
			}
			out = append(out, fetchRequest{url: u, source: src, hasSource: hasSrc})
		}
		return out, true, nil
	}
	u, _, err := a.String("url")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	}
	src, hasSrc, err := a.String("source")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	}
	if u == "" {
		return nil, false, nil
	}
	return []fetchRequest{{url: u, source: src, hasSource: hasSrc}}, false, nil
}

// fetchInt mirrors z.coerce.number().int().min(lo).max(hi).optional(); def when absent.
func fetchInt(a Args, key string, def, lo, hi int64) (int64, error) {
	f, ok, err := a.Number(key)
	if err != nil || !ok {
		return def, err
	}
	if f != math.Trunc(f) || math.IsInf(f, 0) {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("%s: expected integer", key)
	}
	if f < float64(lo) || f > float64(hi) {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("%s: must be between %d and %d", key, lo, hi)
	}
	return int64(f), nil
}

// fetchOne is the cache check plus fetch, S:3288-3370. It writes nothing.
// ttl < 0 means "not given".
func fetchOne(ctx context.Context, d *Deps, r fetchRequest, force bool, ttl int64) fetchJob {
	label := fetchCacheKey(r.source, r.hasSource, r.url)
	if !force && ttl != 0 {
		src, err := d.Store.SourceByLabel(d.Cfg.ProjectKey, label)
		if err == nil && src != nil {
			ttlMs := ttl
			if ttlMs < 0 {
				ttlMs = fetchTTLDefaultMs
			}
			if age := d.Now().Sub(src.IndexedAt).Milliseconds(); age < ttlMs {
				return fetchJob{out: fetchOutcome{kind: "cached", url: r.url, label: src.Label, chunks: src.ChunkCount, age: fetchAge(age), ttl: fetchTTL(ttlMs)}}
			}
		}
	}
	res, err := d.Fetch.Fetch(ctx, r.url)
	if err != nil {
		reason := "exit"
		if errors.Is(err, fetch.ErrEmpty) {
			reason = "empty"
		}
		return fetchJob{out: fetchOutcome{kind: "error", url: r.url, err: err.Error(), reason: reason}}
	}
	return fetchJob{body: res}
}

// fetchIndex is indexFetched, S:3378-3409: route by content kind, one store write.
func fetchIndex(d *Deps, r fetchRequest, res fetch.Result) fetchOutcome {
	var chunks []store.Chunk
	switch res.Kind {
	case fetch.KindJSON:
		var err error
		if chunks, err = store.ChunkJSON(res.Body, store.ChunkMaxBytes); err != nil || len(chunks) == 0 {
			chunks = store.ChunkPlainText(res.Body, 20)
		}
	case fetch.KindHTML:
		chunks = store.ChunkMarkdown(res.Body, store.ChunkMaxBytes)
	default:
		chunks = store.ChunkPlainText(res.Body, 20)
	}
	ir, err := d.Store.Index(d.Cfg.ProjectKey, fetchCacheKey(r.source, r.hasSource, r.url), chunks)
	if err != nil {
		return fetchOutcome{kind: "error", url: r.url, err: err.Error(), reason: "throw"}
	}
	return fetchOutcome{kind: "fetched", url: r.url, label: ir.Label, chunks: ir.TotalChunks, bytes: len(res.Body), preview: fetchPreview(res.Body)}
}
```

`cmd/cmgo/serve_test.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

// rpcPeer drives serve() over in-process pipes with newline-delimited JSON-RPC.
type rpcPeer struct {
	t    *testing.T
	in   *io.PipeWriter
	out  *bufio.Reader
	done chan int
	errb *bytes.Buffer
}

func startServe(t *testing.T, env map[string]string) *rpcPeer {
	t.Helper()
	inR, inW := io.Pipe()
	outR, outW := io.Pipe()
	errb := &bytes.Buffer{}
	p := &rpcPeer{t: t, in: inW, out: bufio.NewReader(outR), done: make(chan int, 1), errb: errb}
	go func() {
		p.done <- serve(context.Background(), serveIO{
			getenv: func(k string) string { return env[k] },
			getwd:  os.Getwd,
			in:     inR,
			out:    outW,
			stderr: errb,
		})
		outW.Close()
	}()
	return p
}

func (p *rpcPeer) send(msg map[string]any) {
	p.t.Helper()
	b, _ := json.Marshal(msg)
	written := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { _, err := p.in.Write(append(b, '\n')); written <- err }()
	select {
	case err := <-written:
		if err != nil {
			p.t.Fatal(err)
		}
	case code := <-p.done:
		p.t.Fatalf("server exited with %d before reading the request (stderr: %s)", code, p.errb.String())
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		p.t.Fatalf("server did not read the request within 10s (stderr: %s)", p.errb.String())
	}
}

func (p *rpcPeer) recv(id int) map[string]any {
	p.t.Helper()
	for {
		line, err := p.out.ReadBytes('\n')
		if err != nil {
			p.t.Fatalf("read: %v (stderr: %s)", err, p.errb.String())
		}
		var m map[string]any
		if err := json.Unmarshal(line, &m); err != nil {
			p.t.Fatalf("bad frame %q: %v", line, err)
		}
		if got, ok := m["id"].(float64); ok && int(got) == id {
			return m
		}
	}
}

func (p *rpcPeer) initialize() {
	p.send(map[string]any{"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "initialize", "params": map[string]any{
		"protocolVersion": "2025-06-18", "capabilities": map[string]any{}, "clientInfo": map[string]any{"name": "t", "version": "0"},
	}})
	if r := p.recv(1); r["error"] != nil {
		p.t.Fatalf("initialize: %v", r["error"])
	}
	p.send(map[string]any{"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "notifications/initialized"})
}

func testEnv(t *testing.T) map[string]string {
	root := t.TempDir()
	project := filepath.Join(root, "project")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(project, 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return map[string]string{"HOME": filepath.Join(root, "home"), "CMGO_DIR": filepath.Join(root, "data"), "CMGO_PROJECT_DIR": project}
}

func TestServeListsFiveToolsAndExitsOnEOF(t *testing.T) {
	env := testEnv(t)
	p := startServe(t, env)
	p.initialize()
	p.send(map[string]any{"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 2, "method": "tools/list"})
	r := p.recv(2)
	var names []string
	for _, tool := range r["result"].(map[string]any)["tools"].([]any) {
		names = append(names, tool.(map[string]any)["name"].(string))
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	want := "ctx_batch_execute,ctx_execute,ctx_execute_file,ctx_fetch_and_index,ctx_search"
	if strings.Join(names, ",") != want {
		t.Fatalf("tools = %v", names)
	}
	p.in.Close()
	select {
	case code := <-p.done:
		if code != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("exit %d: %s", code, p.errb.String())
		}
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("serve did not exit after stdin EOF")
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(env["CMGO_DIR"], "cmgo.db")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("db not created: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestServeFailsOnUnusableDataDir(t *testing.T) {
	env := testEnv(t)
	blocker := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "file")
	os.WriteFile(blocker, []byte("x"), 0o644)
	env["CMGO_DIR"] = filepath.Join(blocker, "sub") // parent is a file
	p := startServe(t, env)
	select {
	case code := <-p.done:
		if code != 1 || !strings.Contains(p.errb.String(), "cmgo: open") {
			t.Fatalf("exit %d stderr %q", code, p.errb.String())
		}
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("serve should fail fast")
	}
}

// pgrepCount counts processes whose command line contains pattern.
func pgrepCount(t *testing.T, pattern string) int {
	t.Helper()
	out, _ := exec.Command("pgrep", "-f", pattern).Output()
	return len(strings.Fields(string(out)))
}

// Review focus: Claude Code closing a session while a foreground command is
// still running must not leave that command (or its children) behind.
func TestServeShutdownKillsForegroundRun(t *testing.T) {
	env := testEnv(t)
	p := startServe(t, env)
	p.initialize()
	// Built at runtime so no other command line (e.g. the shell running go test) contains it.
	marker := fmt.Sprintf("sleep %d", 4000+os.Getpid()%5000)
	p.send(map[string]any{"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 9, "method": "tools/call", "params": map[string]any{
		"name": "ctx_execute", "arguments": map[string]any{"language": "shell", "code": marker + " & " + marker + "; wait"},
	}})
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for pgrepCount(t, marker) < 2 && time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	if pgrepCount(t, marker) < 2 {
		t.Fatal("command never started")
	}
	p.in.Close()
	select {
	case <-p.done:
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("serve did not exit while a foreground command was running")
	}
	deadline = time.Now().Add(3 * time.Second)
	for pgrepCount(t, marker) > 0 && time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	if n := pgrepCount(t, marker); n > 0 {
		exec.Command("pkill", "-f", marker).Run()
		t.Fatalf("%d process(es) left running after shutdown", n)
	}
}

// Review focus: two sessions on the same project share one DB; concurrent
// indexing from both must not surface SQLITE_BUSY to the model.
func TestServeConcurrentSessionsSameDB(t *testing.T) {
	env := testEnv(t)
	a, b := startServe(t, env), startServe(t, env)
	a.initialize()
	b.initialize()
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	errs := make(chan string, 20)
	for _, p := range []*rpcPeer{a, b} {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(p *rpcPeer) {
			defer wg.Done()
			for i := 0; i < 10; i++ {
				id := 100 + i
				p.send(map[string]any{"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": id, "method": "tools/call", "params": map[string]any{
					"name": "ctx_batch_execute", "arguments": map[string]any{
						"commands": []any{map[string]any{"label": fmt.Sprintf("seq%d", i), "command": "seq 1 2000"}},
						"queries":  []any{"1999"},
					},
				}})
				r := p.recv(id)
				res, _ := r["result"].(map[string]any)
				if r["error"] != nil || res == nil || res["isError"] == true {
					errs <- fmt.Sprintf("call %d: %v %v", i, r["error"], res)
				}
			}
		}(p)
	}
	wg.Wait()
	close(errs)
	for e := range errs {
		t.Error(e)
	}
	a.in.Close()
	b.in.Close()
	<-a.done
	<-b.done
}
```

```go
package mcpserver

import (
	"context"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestServerRegistersFiveTools(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t, nil)
	res, err := h.Session.ListTools(context.Background(), nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var names []string
	for _, tool := range res.Tools {
		names = append(names, tool.Name)
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	want := []string{"ctx_batch_execute", "ctx_execute", "ctx_execute_file", "ctx_fetch_and_index", "ctx_search"}
	if strings.Join(names, ",") != strings.Join(want, ",") {
		t.Fatalf("tools = %v, want %v", names, want)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`cmd/cmgo/serve.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/config"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/executor"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/fetch"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/mcpserver"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/security"
	"github.com/lang315/context-mode-go/internal/store"
	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
)

const (
	sourceRetention = 14 * 24 * time.Hour // spec §2.1 SOURCE_RETENTION
	cleanupEvery    = 24 * time.Hour      // spec §2.2: at most once per 24 h
)

// serveIO is what `cmgo serve` talks over; tests pass pipes.
type serveIO struct {
	getenv func(string) string
	getwd  func() (string, error)
	in     io.ReadCloser
	out    io.WriteCloser
	stderr io.Writer
}

// serve runs the MCP server until stdin closes or SIGINT/SIGTERM arrives,
// then terminates background process groups and closes the DB.
func serve(ctx context.Context, sio serveIO) int {
	cfg, err := config.Load(sio.getenv, sio.getwd)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintf(sio.stderr, "cmgo: config: %v\n", err)
		return 1
	}
	st, err := store.Open(cfg.DBPath)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintf(sio.stderr, "cmgo: open %s: %v\n", cfg.DBPath, err)
		return 1
	}
	defer st.Close()
	if _, err := st.CleanupIfDue(time.Now(), sourceRetention, cleanupEvery); err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintf(sio.stderr, "cmgo: cleanup skipped: %v\n", err) // never fatal
	}
	ex := executor.New(cfg.ProjectDir)
	defer ex.Shutdown()

	d := &mcpserver.Deps{
		Cfg:    cfg,
		Store:  st,
		Exec:   ex,
		Policy: security.NewPolicy(cfg.ProjectDir, cfg.Home),
		Fetch:  fetch.New(cfg.FetchStrict),
	}
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(ctx, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer stop()
	// Lift the 16 MB frame cap: TS accepted any size of tool argument.
	t := &mcp.IOTransport{Reader: sio.in, Writer: sio.out, MaxLineLength: -1}
	if err := mcpserver.New(d, version).Run(ctx, t); err != nil && ctx.Err() == nil && err != io.EOF {
		fmt.Fprintf(sio.stderr, "cmgo: serve: %v\n", err)
		return 1
	}
	return 0
}
```

`cmd/cmgo/main.go` (replace the file):

```go
// Command cmgo is a Go rewrite of context-mode for Claude Code.
package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
)

// version is set at build time: go build -ldflags "-X main.version=v0.1.0".
var version = "dev"

func main() {
	os.Exit(run(os.Args[1:], os.Stdout, os.Stderr))
}

func run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int {
	if len(args) == 0 {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "usage: cmgo <serve|version>")
		return 2
	}
	switch args[0] {
	case "version", "--version":
		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "cmgo "+version)
		return 0
	case "serve":
		return serve(context.Background(), serveIO{
			getenv: os.Getenv, getwd: os.Getwd, in: os.Stdin, out: os.Stdout, stderr: stderr,
		})
	default:
		fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "cmgo: unknown command %q\n", args[0])
		return 2
	}
}
```
